## core/data/channels.d.ts
import { Log, type Relations } from './history.ts';
type Key = string | number;
export interface ColumnChannel<D> {
    value: string;
    log?: Log | ((d: D) => Log | null | undefined);
    key?: (d: D) => Key | readonly Key[] | null | undefined;
}
export interface CountChannel {
    relations: Relations;
    count: true;
}
export interface MembersChannel {
    relations: Relations;
    members: {
        agg: 'sum' | 'mean' | 'min' | 'max';
        value: string;
        log?: Log;
    };
}
export interface FormulaChannel<D> {
    formula: (v: Record<string, number>, d: D) => number;
    inputs?: Record<string, Channel<D>>;
}
export type Channel<D> = number | ((d: D) => number) | string | ColumnChannel<D> | CountChannel | MembersChannel | FormulaChannel<D>;
export interface DeriveOptions<D> {
    log?: Log | null;
    id?: (d: D, i: number) => Key;
    place?: (d: D) => readonly [number, number] | null | undefined;
}
export declare const isCount: (c: unknown) => c is CountChannel;
export declare const isMembers: (c: unknown) => c is MembersChannel;
export declare const isFormula: <D>(c: unknown) => c is FormulaChannel<D>;
export declare const isColumn: <D>(c: unknown) => c is ColumnChannel<D>;
export declare function logsOf<D>(chs: Record<string, Channel<D>>, log?: Log | null, items?: readonly D[]): Log[];
export declare class Deriver<D> {
    readonly names: string[];
    remade: number;
    constructor(chs0: Record<string, Channel<D>>, o?: DeriveOptions<D>);
    derive(items: readonly D[]): Log;
}
export declare function derive<D>(items: readonly D[], chs: Record<string, Channel<D>>, o?: DeriveOptions<D>): Log;
export declare const channels: {
    derive: typeof derive;
    logsOf: typeof logsOf;
    Deriver: typeof Deriver;
};
export {};

## core/data/epochs.d.ts
import type { State } from '../world/state.ts';
import { Source, type SourceContext } from './sources/source.ts';
export type Time = number | Date | string;
export type GridSource = Float32Array | {
    values: Float32Array;
    size?: [number, number];
    area?: [number, number, number, number];
};
export type EpochSource<T> = T | (() => T | Promise<T>);
export interface Epoch<T> {
    at: Time;
    source: EpochSource<T>;
}
export interface EpochsOptions {
    blend?: 'step' | 'morph';
    by?: string;
    keep?: number;
    id?: string;
}
export interface EpochPick {
    i: number;
    j: number;
    f: number;
}
export interface EpochStats {
    id: string;
    epochs: number;
    held: number;
    bytes: number;
    loading: number;
    loads: number;
    dropped: number;
    shown: [number, number, number];
}
export declare class Epochs<T = unknown> extends Source {
    readonly blend: 'step' | 'morph';
    readonly by: string;
    readonly times: readonly number[];
    constructor(list: readonly Epoch<T>[], o?: EpochsOptions);
    get from(): Date;
    get to(): Date;
    get length(): number;
    pick(t: Time): EpochPick;
    get now(): EpochPick;
    value(i: number): T | undefined;
    timeOf(s: State): number;
    update(s: State): boolean;
    follow(t: number, rate?: number): boolean;
    epoch(k: number): T | Promise<T> | undefined;
    shown(): {
        a: T | undefined;
        b: T | undefined;
        f: number;
        i: number;
        j: number;
    };
    onArrive(fn: () => void): () => void;
    epochStats(): EpochStats;
    status(): import("./sources/source.ts").SourceStatus;
    protected load(_ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    frame(s: State, _ctx: SourceContext): void;
}
export declare function gridOf(v: unknown): {
    values: Float32Array;
    size?: [number, number];
} | null;
export declare const isEpochs: (v: unknown) => v is Epochs;
export declare const epochs: <T = unknown>(list: readonly Epoch<T>[], o?: EpochsOptions) => Epochs<T>;

## core/data/features.d.ts
import type { BBox } from '../space/geo.ts';
export type Position = [number, number] | [number, number, number];
export type Geometry = {
    type: 'Point';
    coordinates: Position;
} | {
    type: 'MultiPoint';
    coordinates: Position[];
} | {
    type: 'LineString';
    coordinates: Position[];
} | {
    type: 'MultiLineString';
    coordinates: Position[][];
} | {
    type: 'Polygon';
    coordinates: Position[][];
} | {
    type: 'MultiPolygon';
    coordinates: Position[][][];
};
export type Props = Record<string, any>;
export interface Feature<P = Props> {
    id: string | number;
    props: P;
    geometry: Geometry | null;
    cuts?: Uint8Array[][];
}
export type IdRule = string | ((f: {
    id?: unknown;
    properties?: Props | null;
    [k: string]: unknown;
}, i: number) => string | number);
export declare function normalize(input: unknown, o?: {
    id?: IdRule;
    where?: string;
}): Feature[];
export declare function checkGeometry(g: unknown, where: string): Geometry | null;
export declare function polygonsOf(g: Geometry | null): Position[][][];
export declare function linesOf(g: Geometry | null, rings?: boolean): Position[][];
export declare function pointsOf(g: Geometry | null, centres?: boolean): Position[];
export declare function bboxOf(g: Geometry | null, into?: BBox): BBox | null;
export declare function centreOf(g: Geometry | null): Position | null;
export declare function ringArea(r: Position[]): number;
export declare function pointInRing(p: Position, r: Position[]): boolean;
export declare function anchorOf(g: Geometry | null): Position | null;
export declare function contains(g: Geometry | null, p: Position): boolean;

## core/data/history.d.ts
import type { Loader } from './loading/loader.ts';
export type PlaceType = 'u16' | 'f32';
export type TimeType = 'u16' | 'f32';
export type ValueType = 'u8' | 'f32';
export interface ValueSpec {
    type?: ValueType;
    domain?: [number, number];
    step?: boolean;
}
export interface AltitudeSpec extends ValueSpec {
    over?: 'ground' | 'sea';
    value?: string;
}
export interface LogOptions {
    id?: string;
    values?: Record<string, ValueSpec | ValueType>;
    place?: {
        type?: PlaceType;
        box?: [number, number, number, number];
    };
    time?: {
        type?: TimeType;
        epoch?: number | Date | string;
        step?: number;
    };
    keep?: number;
    url?: string;
    travel?: boolean;
    altitude?: 'ground' | 'sea' | AltitudeSpec;
    attribution?: string;
}
export interface StateAt {
    id: string;
    index: number;
    lon: number;
    lat: number;
    heading: number;
    speed: number;
    values: Record<string, number>;
    props: Record<string, unknown>;
    outside: -1 | 0 | 1;
    alt?: number;
}
export interface Columns {
    ids: readonly (string | number)[];
    count: ArrayLike<number>;
    start?: ArrayLike<number>;
    t: ArrayLike<number>;
    lon?: ArrayLike<number>;
    lat?: ArrayLike<number>;
    lonlat?: ArrayLike<number>;
    values?: Record<string, ArrayLike<number>>;
    alt?: ArrayLike<number>;
    props?: readonly (Record<string, unknown> | undefined)[];
}
export type LogChange = {
    kind: 'samples';
    lo: number;
    hi: number;
} | {
    kind: 'thing';
    index: number;
} | {
    kind: 'all';
};
export declare class Log {
    readonly id: string;
    attribution: string;
    epoch: number;
    readonly timeType: TimeType;
    readonly timeStep: number;
    readonly placeType: PlaceType;
    lon0: number;
    lat0: number;
    m0: number;
    box: [number, number, number, number];
    readonly values: {
        name: string;
        type: ValueType;
        domain: [number, number];
        step?: boolean;
    }[];
    readonly keep: number;
    readonly travel: boolean;
    readonly altitude: {
        over: 'ground' | 'sea';
        value: string;
    } | null;
    altMax: number;
    ids: string[];
    props: Record<string, unknown>[];
    first: Int32Array<ArrayBuffer>;
    count: Int32Array<ArrayBuffer>;
    cap: Int32Array<ArrayBuffer>;
    head: Int32Array<ArrayBuffer>;
    stride: Int32Array<ArrayBuffer>;
    seen: [number, number, number, number] | null;
    slots: number;
    used: number;
    time: Uint16Array | Float32Array;
    place: Uint16Array | Float32Array;
    cols: (Uint8Array | Float32Array)[];
    maxSpeed: number;
    from: number;
    to: number;
    version: number;
    readonly url: string | null;
    failure: Error | null;
    constructor(o?: LogOptions);
    rel(lon: number, lat: number): [number, number];
    lonLat(x: number, y: number): [number, number];
    relAt(slot: number, out?: [number, number]): [number, number];
    secAt(slot: number): number;
    valueAt(k: number, slot: number): number;
    slot(i: number, j: number): number;
    get size(): number;
    get samples(): number;
    indexOf(id: string | number): number;
    get bytes(): number;
    append(id: string | number, t: number | Date | string, lon: number, lat: number, values?: Record<string, number>, props?: Record<string, unknown>): boolean;
    altK(): number;
    appendMany(rows: Iterable<{
        id: string | number;
        t: number | Date | string;
        lon: number;
        lat: number;
        alt?: number;
        values?: Record<string, number>;
        props?: Record<string, unknown>;
    }>): number;
    watch(fn: (c: LogChange) => void): () => void;
    sampleAt(i: number, sec: number): number;
    at(id: string | number | number, t: number | Date | string, byIndex?: boolean): StateAt | null;
    relOf(i: number, sec: number, out: [number, number] | [number, number, number], hold?: boolean, k?: number): boolean;
    densest(seconds: number): number;
    dense: [number, number][];
    densestOf(i: number, seconds: number): number;
    bounds(): [number, number, number, number] | null;
    headingAt(i: number, j: number): number;
    stateAt(t: number | Date | string, ids?: readonly (string | number)[]): StateAt[];
    dateOf(sec: number): Date;
    toBytes(o?: {
        things?: number;
    }): Uint8Array;
    static fromColumns(c: Columns, o?: LogOptions): Log;
    static fromBytes(buf: ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array, o?: {
        keep?: number;
        travel?: boolean;
        altitude?: LogOptions['altitude'];
    }): Log;
    start(ctx: {
        loader: Loader;
        error?: (e: unknown) => void;
    }): Promise<void>;
    get ready(): Promise<void>;
    get loading(): boolean;
}
export declare function smallest(o: {
    box: [number, number, number, number];
    from: number | Date | string;
    to: number | Date | string;
    precision?: {
        place?: number;
        time?: number;
    };
}): {
    place: {
        type: PlaceType;
        box?: [number, number, number, number];
    };
    time: {
        type: TimeType;
        epoch: number;
        step?: number;
    };
};
export interface ClockWorld {
    time: Date;
    readonly timeRate: number;
    setTime(t: Date | 'now', o?: {
        rate?: number;
    }): unknown;
    setTimeRate(rate: number): unknown;
    on(ev: 'frame', f: (e: {
        dt: number;
    }) => void): () => void;
}
export interface ReplayOptions {
    from?: number | Date | string;
    to?: number | Date | string;
    speed?: number;
    loop?: boolean;
    play?: boolean;
}
export interface Playback {
    readonly playing: boolean;
    readonly speed: number;
    readonly from: Date;
    readonly to: Date;
    readonly time: Date;
    readonly progress: number;
    play(): Playback;
    pause(): Playback;
    toggle(): Playback;
    setSpeed(x: number): Playback;
    seek(t: number | Date | string, o?: {
        share?: boolean;
    }): Playback;
    on(fn: (p: Playback, why: 'play' | 'pause' | 'seek' | 'speed' | 'loop' | 'end') => void): () => void;
    dispose(): void;
}
export declare function replay(w: ClockWorld, o?: ReplayOptions & {
    log?: Log;
}): Playback;
export interface RelationRow {
    of: string | number;
    to: string | number;
    from: number | Date | string;
    until: number | Date | string;
    kind?: string;
}
export interface Held {
    of: string;
    to: string;
    from: number;
    until: number;
    kind: string;
}
export interface RelationsOptions<R> {
    of?: (r: R) => string | number;
    to?: (r: R) => string | number;
    from?: (r: R) => number | Date | string;
    until?: (r: R) => number | Date | string;
    kind?: (r: R) => string;
}
export declare class Relations {
    readonly rows: Held[];
    version: number;
    constructor(rows: readonly Held[]);
    set(rows: readonly Held[]): this;
    windowsOf(id: string | number): [number, number][];
    membersOf(host: string | number): readonly Held[];
    hostOf(id: string | number, t: number | Date | string): string | null;
    heldBy(host: string | number, t: number | Date | string): string[];
    get count(): {
        relations: Relations;
        count: true;
    };
    members(agg: 'sum' | 'mean' | 'min' | 'max', value: string, log?: Log): {
        relations: Relations;
        members: {
            agg: 'sum' | 'mean' | 'min' | 'max';
            value: string;
            log?: Log;
        };
    };
    static of<R = RelationRow>(rows: readonly R[], o?: RelationsOptions<R>): Relations;
    static fromLog(log: Log, o: {
        value: string;
        to: (v: number, thing: string) => string | number | null | undefined;
        kind?: string;
    }): Relations;
}
export declare const history: {
    log: (o?: LogOptions) => Log;
    fromBytes: (b: ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array, o?: Parameters<typeof Log.fromBytes>[1]) => Log;
    fromColumns: (c: Columns, o?: LogOptions) => Log;
    smallest: typeof smallest;
    relations: (<R = RelationRow>(rows: readonly R[], o?: RelationsOptions<R>) => Relations) & {
        fromLog: (log: Log, o: {
            value: string;
            to: (v: number, thing: string) => string | number | null | undefined;
            kind?: string;
        }) => Relations;
    };
};

## core/data/loading/loader.d.ts
import { AslnutError } from '../../world/errors.ts';
import { type Pack } from './pack.ts';
import { type Capability, type Terms } from '../rights.ts';
import { type ScopeInfo, type Store } from './stores.ts';
export type Kind = 'bytes' | 'json' | 'text' | 'image' | 'imageRaw' | 'pixels' | 'imagePixels' | 'entry';
export type Priority = 'now' | 'soon' | 'later';
export type Policy = 'cacheFirst' | 'networkFirst' | 'cacheOnly' | 'networkOnly';
export type Mode = 'live' | 'record' | 'replay' | 'offline';
export type Tier = 'memory' | 'shared' | 'pack' | 'store' | 'network';
export interface SourceRule {
    match?: RegExp | string | ((url: string) => boolean);
    policy?: Policy;
    terms?: Partial<Terms>;
    store?: 'session' | 'scope' | 'persist';
    concurrency?: number;
    maxAge?: number;
}
export interface Req {
    url: string;
    range?: [number, number];
}
export interface GetOptions {
    kind?: Kind;
    priority?: Priority;
    source?: string;
    scope?: string;
    keep?: boolean;
    prefetch?: boolean;
    init?: RequestInit;
    signal?: AbortSignal;
}
export declare const isAbort: (e: unknown) => boolean;
export interface LoaderOptions {
    scope?: string;
    mode?: Mode;
    memory?: number | string;
    evict?: 'least-seen' | ((item: {
        key: string;
        bytes: number;
        seenMsAgo: number;
    }) => number);
    store?: Store | null;
    sources?: Record<string, SourceRule>;
    concurrency?: number;
    retries?: number;
    timeout?: number;
    maxAge?: number;
    packs?: Pack[];
    fetch?: typeof fetch;
}
export interface LoadEvent {
    url: string;
    kind: Kind;
    tier: Tier;
    source: string;
    bytes: number;
    ms: number;
}
export interface MissEvent {
    url: string;
    mode: Mode;
    source: string;
    scope: string;
}
export interface RefuseEvent {
    url: string;
    source: string;
    capability: Capability;
}
export interface Manifest {
    scope: string;
    created: number;
    bytes: number;
    items: {
        url: string;
        bytes: number;
        source: string;
        status: number;
    }[];
}
export interface Coverage {
    have: number;
    total: number;
    fraction: number;
    missing: string[];
    absent: string[];
}
export interface EnsureResult {
    fetched: number;
    already: number;
    absent: number;
    failed: number;
    refused: number;
    bytes: number;
    problems: {
        url: string;
        why: string;
    }[];
}
type Events = {
    load: LoadEvent;
    miss: MissEvent;
    refuse: RefuseEvent;
    evict: {
        key: string;
        bytes: number;
    };
    progress: {
        phase: string;
        done: number;
        total: number;
        bytes: number;
    };
    error: {
        url: string;
        error: string;
    };
};
export declare class LoaderMiss extends AslnutError {
    readonly url: string;
    readonly mode: Mode;
    constructor(url: string, mode: Mode);
}
export declare const DEFAULT_SOURCES: Record<string, SourceRule>;
export declare class Loader {
    scope: string;
    readonly store: Store | null;
    budget: number;
    constructor(o?: LoaderOptions);
    private static blankStats;
    get mode(): Mode;
    set mode(m: Mode);
    source(id: string, r: SourceRule): this;
    ruleOf(url: string, id?: string): [string, SourceRule];
    termsOf(url: string, source?: string): Terms;
    can(cap: Capability, url: string, source?: string): boolean;
    keyOf(req: string | Req): string;
    get<T = unknown>(req: string | Req, o?: GetOptions): Promise<T | null>;
    objectURL(url: string, type?: string): Promise<string>;
    readScopes(scope?: string): string[];
    read(scope: string): this;
    setMemory(memory: number | string, evict?: LoaderOptions['evict']): this;
    forget(): void;
    addPack(p: Pack): this;
    removePack(name: string): this;
    get packList(): Pack[];
    export(scope?: string): Promise<{
        blob: Blob;
        items: number;
        refused: string[];
    }>;
    import(file: Blob | ArrayBuffer, o?: {
        as?: 'pack' | 'store';
        scope?: string;
        name?: string;
    }): Promise<{
        scope: string;
        items: number;
    }>;
    scopes(): Promise<ScopeInfo[]>;
    scopeRules(name: string, r: {
        persist?: boolean;
        maxAge?: number;
    }): Promise<void>;
    pin(scope: string, on?: boolean): Promise<void>;
    clear(scope?: string): Promise<number>;
    coverage(list: string[] | Manifest, scope?: string): Promise<Coverage>;
    ensure(list: string[] | Manifest, o?: {
        scope?: string;
        concurrency?: number;
        onProgress?: (done: number, total: number, bytes: number) => void;
    }): Promise<EnsureResult>;
    manifest(scope?: string): Promise<Manifest>;
    stats(): {
        mode: Mode;
        scope: string;
        tiers: {
            memory: number;
            network: number;
            pack: number;
            shared: number;
            store: number;
        };
        bytesNetwork: number;
        misses: number;
        errors: number;
        kept: number;
        refused: number;
        inflight: number;
        memory: {
            bytes: number;
            items: number;
            budget: number;
        };
        byKind: Record<string, {
            count: number;
            bytes: number;
        }>;
        bySource: Record<string, {
            count: number;
            network: number;
            bytes: number;
        }>;
        packs: string[];
    };
    resetStats(): void;
    on<K extends keyof Events>(ev: K, f: (e: Events[K]) => void): () => void;
    dispose(): void;
}
export declare const loader: (o?: LoaderOptions) => Loader;
export {};

## core/data/loading/pack.d.ts
import { type Entry } from './stores.ts';
export interface Pack {
    readonly name: string;
    get(key: string): Promise<Entry | null>;
    has(key: string): Promise<boolean>;
    keys?(): string[];
}
export declare function writePack(scope: string, entries: {
    key: string;
    e: Entry;
}[]): Blob;
export declare class FilePack implements Pack {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly scope: string;
    readonly created: number;
    static read(blob: Blob | ArrayBuffer, name?: string): Promise<FilePack>;
    entries(): {
        key: string;
        e: Entry;
    }[];
    get(key: string): Promise<Entry | null>;
    has(key: string): Promise<boolean>;
    keys(): string[];
    get bytes(): number;
}

## core/data/loading/stores.d.ts
export interface Entry {
    status: number;
    type: string;
    data: ArrayBuffer;
    source: string;
    at: number;
    meta?: Record<string, string>;
}
export interface ScopeInfo {
    name: string;
    created: number;
    at: number;
    bytes: number;
    items: number;
    pinned?: boolean;
    persist?: boolean;
    maxAge?: number;
}
export interface Store {
    readonly kind: 'cache' | 'folder' | 'memory';
    names(): Promise<string[]>;
    get(scope: string, key: string): Promise<Entry | null>;
    has(scope: string, key: string): Promise<boolean>;
    head(scope: string, key: string): Promise<{
        bytes: number;
        source: string;
        status: number;
    } | null>;
    put(scope: string, key: string, e: Entry): Promise<void>;
    keys(scope: string): Promise<string[]>;
    info(scope: string): Promise<ScopeInfo | null>;
    setInfo(scope: string, patch: Partial<ScopeInfo>): Promise<void>;
    drop(scope: string): Promise<void>;
}
export declare function checkScope(scope: string): string;
export declare const blankInfo: (name: string) => ScopeInfo;
export declare class CacheStore implements Store {
    readonly kind: 'cache';
    names(): Promise<string[]>;
    get(scope: string, key: string): Promise<Entry | null>;
    has(scope: string, key: string): Promise<boolean>;
    head(scope: string, key: string): Promise<{
        bytes: number;
        source: string;
        status: number;
    } | null>;
    put(scope: string, key: string, e: Entry): Promise<void>;
    keys(scope: string): Promise<string[]>;
    info(scope: string): Promise<ScopeInfo | null>;
    setInfo(scope: string, patch: Partial<ScopeInfo>): Promise<void>;
    drop(scope: string): Promise<void>;
    flush(): Promise<void>;
    close(): void;
}
export declare class MemoryStore implements Store {
    readonly kind: 'memory';
    names(): Promise<string[]>;
    get(scope: string, key: string): Promise<Entry | null>;
    has(scope: string, key: string): Promise<boolean>;
    head(scope: string, key: string): Promise<{
        bytes: number;
        source: string;
        status: number;
    } | null>;
    put(scope: string, key: string, e: Entry): Promise<void>;
    keys(scope: string): Promise<string[]>;
    info(scope: string): Promise<ScopeInfo | null>;
    setInfo(scope: string, patch: Partial<ScopeInfo>): Promise<void>;
    drop(scope: string): Promise<void>;
}

## core/data/rights.d.ts
export interface Terms {
    show: boolean;
    prefetch: boolean;
    keep: boolean;
    export: boolean;
}
export declare const SHOW_ONLY: Readonly<Terms>;
export declare const OPEN: Readonly<Terms>;

## core/data/scenarios.d.ts
import type { Element } from '../world/element.ts';
import type { Loader } from './loading/loader.ts';
import type { Feature } from './features.ts';
import { Source } from './sources/source.ts';
import type { Kernel, Rule } from '../simulation/rules.ts';
export type RuleLike = Rule | Kernel | ((cur: number, n: {
    laplacian: number;
    neighbours: [number, number, number, number];
    inputs: Record<string, number>;
}, dt: number, p: Record<string, number>) => number);
export interface ScenarioOptions {
    base?: string;
    set?: Record<string, Source | Feature[]>;
    params?: Record<string, unknown>;
    rules?: Record<string, RuleLike | {
        rule: RuleLike;
        params?: Record<string, number>;
    }>;
    label?: string;
}
export interface Scenario {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly base: string;
    readonly set: Record<string, Source | Feature[]>;
    readonly params: Record<string, unknown>;
    readonly rules: ScenarioOptions['rules'] & object;
    readonly label: string;
}
export interface ScenarioDiff {
    added: {
        source: string;
        id: string | number;
    }[];
    removed: {
        source: string;
        id: string | number;
    }[];
    changed: {
        source: string;
        id: string | number;
    }[];
    params: {
        key: string;
        a: unknown;
        b: unknown;
    }[];
    rules: {
        field: string;
        a: string;
        b: string;
    }[];
}
export type CompareView = 'split' | 'swipe' | 'difference';
export interface ScenarioWorld {
    readonly elements: readonly Element[];
    readonly loader: Loader;
    add(...l: Element[]): unknown;
    remove(id: string): unknown;
    element(id: string): Element | undefined;
    get(key: string): unknown;
    set(key: string, v: unknown): unknown;
    invalidate(): void;
    error(e: unknown): void;
    compareView: {
        at: number;
        leftHides: Set<Element>;
        rightHides: Set<Element>;
    } | null;
    readonly canvasElement?: HTMLCanvasElement | null;
}
export declare class Scenarios {
    constructor(w: ScenarioWorld);
    define(name: string, o?: ScenarioOptions): Scenario;
    list(): string[];
    get current(): string;
    resolved(name: string): {
        set: Record<string, Source | Feature[]>;
        params: Record<string, unknown>;
        rules: Record<string, {
            rule: RuleLike;
            params?: Record<string, number>;
        }>;
    };
    use(name: string): this;
    diff(a: string, b: string): Promise<ScenarioDiff>;
    compare(a: string | null, b?: string, o?: {
        view?: CompareView;
        at?: number;
    }): this;
    setSplit(at: number): this;
    get comparing(): {
        a: string;
        b: string;
        view: CompareView;
    } | null;
}
export type ScenarioApi = ((name: string, o?: ScenarioOptions) => Scenario) & {
    use(name: string): Scenarios;
    diff(a: string, b: string): Promise<ScenarioDiff>;
    compare(a: string | null, b?: string, o?: {
        view?: CompareView;
        at?: number;
    }): Scenarios;
    setSplit(at: number): Scenarios;
    list(): string[];
    readonly current: string;
    readonly comparing: Scenarios['comparing'];
    readonly manager: Scenarios;
};
export declare function scenarioApi(w: ScenarioWorld): ScenarioApi;

## core/data/sources/index.d.ts
import type { State } from '../../world/state.ts';
import { type When } from '../../observation/presence.ts';
import { type Feature, type IdRule, type Props } from '../features.ts';
import { type CSVOptions } from './csv.ts';
import { type Header } from './pmtiles.ts';
import { Source, type SourceContext } from './source.ts';
import { Tiled, type TiledOptions } from './tiled.ts';
import { epochs } from '../epochs.ts';
export { Source, Joined } from './source.ts';
export type { SourceContext, SourceStatus, JoinOptions } from './source.ts';
export { Tiled, gunzip } from './tiled.ts';
export type { TiledOptions } from './tiled.ts';
export { WFS } from './wfs.ts';
export type { WFSOptions, WFSInfo } from './wfs.ts';
export { readXML, readWFSCapabilities, readWFSDescribe, readWMSCapabilities, exceptionOf, epsgOf } from './ogc.ts';
export type { ServiceOptions, WFSCapabilities, WFSLayer, WFSFields, WMSCapabilities, WMSLayer, XNode } from './ogc.ts';
export interface RowOptions {
    id?: IdRule;
    lon?: string;
    lat?: string;
    attribution?: string;
}
export declare class Rows<P = Props> extends Source<P> {
    protected o: RowOptions;
    constructor(rows?: P[], o?: RowOptions & {
        id?: IdRule;
    });
    protected load(_ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    set(rows: P[] | readonly P[] | {
        features: unknown[];
    }): this;
    upsert(rows: P[] | readonly P[]): this;
    patch(id: string | number, change: Partial<P> | {
        props?: Partial<P>;
        geometry?: unknown;
    }): this;
    remove(...ids: (string | number)[]): this;
    rows(): P[];
}
export interface QueryOptions<P> extends RowOptions {
    deps?: (s: State) => unknown;
    when?: When;
    every?: number;
    debounce?: number;
    map?: (answer: unknown) => P[] | unknown;
}
export declare class Query<P = Props> extends Rows<P> {
    answeredFor: unknown;
    constructor(fn: (s: State) => unknown, o?: QueryOptions<P>);
    protected load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    frame(s: State, ctx: SourceContext): void;
    refresh(): Promise<void>;
    status(): import("./source.ts").SourceStatus;
}
export type StreamMessage<P> = P[] | {
    set?: P[];
    upsert?: P[];
    remove?: (string | number)[];
    patch?: {
        id: string | number;
        change: Partial<P>;
    };
};
export interface StreamOptions<P> extends RowOptions {
    url?: string;
    connect?: (emit: (m: StreamMessage<P>) => void) => (() => void) | void;
    parse?: (data: unknown) => StreamMessage<P>;
    retry?: number;
}
export declare class Stream<P = Props> extends Rows<P> {
    messages: number;
    constructor(o: StreamOptions<P>);
    protected load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    reopen(): void;
    close(): void;
}
export interface GeoJSONOptions {
    id?: IdRule;
    attribution?: string;
}
export declare class GeoJSON extends Source {
    constructor(input: string | object, o?: GeoJSONOptions);
    protected load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
}
export declare class CSV extends Rows {
    constructor(input: string, o?: CSVOptions & RowOptions);
    protected load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
}
export declare class Custom<P = Props> extends Rows<P> {
    constructor(o: RowOptions & {
        load: (ctx: SourceContext) => unknown;
    });
    protected load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
}
export type PMTilesOptions = TiledOptions;
export interface TemplateOptions extends TiledOptions {
    minZoom?: number;
    maxZoom?: number;
    bounds?: [number, number, number, number];
}
export declare class PMTiles extends Tiled {
    header: Header | null;
    constructor(url: string, o?: PMTilesOptions);
    protected load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    protected tile(z: number, x: number, y: number): Promise<Feature[] | null>;
}
export declare class TemplateTiles extends Tiled {
    constructor(url: string, format: 'mvt' | 'geojson', o?: TemplateOptions);
    protected load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    protected tile(z: number, x: number, y: number, ctx: SourceContext): Promise<Feature[] | null>;
}
export declare const data: {
    epochs: typeof epochs;
    geojson: (input: string | object, o?: GeoJSONOptions & TemplateOptions) => Source;
    mvt: (url: string, o?: TemplateOptions) => TemplateTiles;
    csv: (input: string, o?: CSVOptions & RowOptions) => CSV;
    pmtiles: (url: string, o?: PMTilesOptions) => PMTiles;
    rows: <P = Props>(rows?: P[], o?: RowOptions) => Rows<P>;
    query: <P = Props>(fn: (s: State) => unknown, o?: QueryOptions<P>) => Query<P>;
    stream: <P = Props>(o: string | StreamOptions<P>) => Stream<P>;
    custom: <P = Props>(o: RowOptions & {
        load: (ctx: SourceContext) => unknown;
    }) => Custom<P>;
    wfs: ((url: string, o: import("./wfs.ts").WFSOptions) => import("./wfs.ts").WFS) & {
        capabilities: (url: string, o?: import("./ogc.ts").ServiceOptions & {
            version?: '2.0.0' | '1.1.0';
        }) => Promise<import("./ogc.ts").WFSCapabilities>;
        describe: (url: string, typeName: string, o?: import("./ogc.ts").ServiceOptions & {
            version?: '2.0.0' | '1.1.0';
        }) => Promise<import("./ogc.ts").WFSFields>;
    };
};

## core/data/sources/pmtiles.d.ts
export declare const Compression: {
    readonly Unknown: 0;
    readonly None: 1;
    readonly Gzip: 2;
    readonly Brotli: 3;
    readonly Zstd: 4;
};
export type Compression = (typeof Compression)[keyof typeof Compression];
export declare const TileType: {
    readonly Unknown: 0;
    readonly Mvt: 1;
    readonly Png: 2;
    readonly Jpeg: 3;
    readonly Webp: 4;
    readonly Avif: 5;
};
export type TileType = (typeof TileType)[keyof typeof TileType];
export interface Header {
    rootOffset: number;
    rootLength: number;
    metadataOffset: number;
    metadataLength: number;
    leafOffset: number;
    leafLength: number;
    dataOffset: number;
    dataLength: number;
    addressed: number;
    entries: number;
    contents: number;
    clustered: boolean;
    internalCompression: Compression;
    tileCompression: Compression;
    tileType: TileType;
    minZoom: number;
    maxZoom: number;
    bounds: [number, number, number, number];
    center: [number, number, number];
}
export interface Entry {
    tileId: number;
    offset: number;
    length: number;
    runLength: number;
}
export declare function tileId(z: number, x: number, y: number): number;
export declare function zxyOf(id: number): [number, number, number];
export declare function encodeDirectory(entries: Entry[]): Uint8Array;
export declare function decodeDirectory(b: Uint8Array): Entry[];
export declare function encodeHeader(h: Header): Uint8Array;
export declare function decodeHeader(b: Uint8Array): Header;
export type Codec = (b: Uint8Array, c: Compression) => Uint8Array | Promise<Uint8Array>;
export interface WriterOptions {
    tileType: TileType;
    tileCompression: Compression;
    metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
    bounds?: [number, number, number, number];
    center?: [number, number, number];
}
export declare class PMTilesWriter {
    constructor(o: WriterOptions);
    add(z: number, x: number, y: number, bytes: Uint8Array): void;
    get size(): number;
    finish(gzip: (b: Uint8Array) => Uint8Array | Promise<Uint8Array>): Promise<Uint8Array>;
}
export type RangeFetch = (offset: number, length: number) => Promise<Uint8Array>;
export declare class PMTilesReader {
    header: Header;
    metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
    constructor(fetch: RangeFetch, codec: Codec);
    open(): Promise<this>;
    tile(z: number, x: number, y: number): Promise<Uint8Array | null>;
}

import type { Loader, Priority } from '../loading/loader.ts';

export type Ctx2D = OffscreenCanvasRenderingContext2D;

    x: number;
    y: number;
}

    loader: Loader;
    priority: Priority;
    prefetch: boolean;
}
export type RasterBlend = 'normal' | 'multiply' | 'screen' | 'add';

    readonly kind: 'imagery';
    id: string;
    attribution: string;
    terms: Terms;
    when?: When;
    where?: BBox;
    minZoom: number;
    maxZoom: number;
    opacity: number;

    urlFor?(tile: RasterTile): string | null;
}

    maxZoom?: number;

}

}

}

        url: string;

    scale?: number;
    legend?: string;

## core/data/sources/source.d.ts
import type { Loader } from '../loading/loader.ts';
import type { State } from '../../world/state.ts';
import type { Feature, Props } from '../features.ts';
export interface SourceContext {
    loader: Loader;
    error(e: unknown): void;
}
export interface SourceStatus {
    pending: string[];
    failed: {
        what: string;
        error: string;
    }[];
}
export interface JoinOptions<P = Props> {
    on: string | ((f: Feature<P>) => unknown);
    rowsOn?: string | ((f: Feature) => unknown);
}
export declare abstract class Source<P = Props> {
    readonly kind: 'source';
    readonly id: string;
    attribution: string;
    version: number;
    shapeVersion: number;
    failure: Error | null;
    protected list: Feature<P>[];
    protected byId: Map<string | number, Feature<P>>;
    constructor(o?: {
        id?: string;
        attribution?: string;
    });
    features(): readonly Feature<P>[];
    get byView(): boolean;
    get(id: string | number): Feature<P> | undefined;
    get size(): number;
    start(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    protected unstart(): void;
    get ready(): Promise<void>;
    protected abstract load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    frame(_s: State, _ctx: SourceContext): void;
    status(): SourceStatus;
    protected track<T>(p: Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
    watch(fn: () => void, o?: {
        now?: boolean;
    }): () => void;
    get watching(): number;
    inputs(): readonly Source[];
    reopen(): void;
    close(): void;
    protected replace(list: Feature<P>[], shapes?: boolean): void;
    protected changed(shapes: boolean): void;
    join<R = Props>(rows: Source<R>, o: JoinOptions<P>): Joined<P, R>;
}
export declare class Joined<P = Props, R = Props> extends Source<P & Partial<R>> {
    readonly shapes: Source<P>;
    readonly rows: Source<R>;
    constructor(shapes: Source<P>, rows: Source<R>, o: JoinOptions<P>);
    inputs(): readonly Source[];
    reopen(): void;
    close(): void;
    features(): readonly Feature<P & Partial<R>>[];
    get(id: string | number): Feature<P & Partial<R>> | undefined;
    get size(): number;
    protected load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    frame(s: State, ctx: SourceContext): void;
    status(): SourceStatus;
    get ready(): Promise<void>;
    get byView(): boolean;
    matched: number;
}

import { Source, type SourceContext } from './source.ts';

        minZoom: number;
        maxZoom: number;
        bounds: [number, number, number, number];

    get byView(): boolean;
    protected abstract tile(z: number, x: number, y: number, ctx: SourceContext): Promise<Feature[] | null>;
    protected levelFor(s: State, h: {
        minZoom: number;
        maxZoom: number;
    }): number;
    protected answerOf(key: string): string;
    protected reach(s: State): [number, number];
    protected reachTight(s: State, more?: number): [number, number];
    protected skip(_s: State): boolean;
    frame(s: State, ctx: SourceContext): void;
    protected need(z: number, box: [number, number, number, number]): void;
    protected mvt(b: Uint8Array, z: number, x: number, y: number, compression: number): Promise<Feature[]>;
    get tileCount(): {
        wanted: number;
        in: number;
        kept: number;
    };
}

## core/data/sources/wfs.d.ts
import type { State } from '../../world/state.ts';
import { type Feature, type IdRule } from '../features.ts';
import { type BBox } from '../../space/geo.ts';
import type { SourceContext, SourceStatus } from './source.ts';
import { Tiled } from './tiled.ts';
import { type ServiceOptions, type WFSCapabilities } from './ogc.ts';
export interface WFSOptions extends ServiceOptions {
    typeName: string;
    version?: '2.0.0' | '1.1.0' | 'auto';
    filter?: string | {
        cql: string;
    } | {
        fes: string;
    };
    geometryName?: string;
    crs?: string;
    format?: string;
    id?: IdRule;
    extent?: BBox;
    most?: number;
    orExtent?: BBox;
    tiles?: boolean;
    gridOffset?: number;
    minGrid?: number;
    maxGrid?: number;
    minZoom?: number;
    pageSize?: number;
    maxFeatures?: number;
    maxTiles?: number;
    bounds?: BBox;
    attribution?: string;
}
export interface WFSInfo {
    version: string;
    crs: string;
    requests: number;
    bytes: number;
    tiles: number;
    truncated: number;
    features: number;
    ms: number;
}
export declare class WFS extends Tiled {
    readonly url: string;
    constructor(url: string, o: WFSOptions);
    protected load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    get byView(): boolean;
    protected reach(s: State): [number, number];
    protected skip(s: State): boolean;
    protected levelFor(s: State, h: {
        minZoom: number;
        maxZoom: number;
    }): number;
    protected answerOf(key: string): string;
    get info(): WFSInfo;
    status(): SourceStatus;
    protected tile(z: number, x: number, y: number, ctx: SourceContext): Promise<Feature[]>;
    urlFor(box: BBox | null, start: number, count: number): Promise<string>;
}
export declare const wfs: ((url: string, o: WFSOptions) => WFS) & {
    capabilities: (url: string, o?: ServiceOptions & {
        version?: '2.0.0' | '1.1.0';
    }) => Promise<WFSCapabilities>;
    describe: (url: string, typeName: string, o?: ServiceOptions & {
        version?: '2.0.0' | '1.1.0';
    }) => Promise<import("./ogc.ts").WFSFields>;
};

import { Element, type AttachContext, type ElementProps, type ElementStatus, type FrameContext, type Picked } from '../../world/element.ts';
import type { State } from '../../world/state.ts';
import type { Loader } from '../../data/loading/loader.ts';
import type { DrawCall, GTexture, Graphics } from '../../render/graphics/interface.ts';
import { type Color } from '../../render/style/color.ts';
import { type SizeOf } from '../../render/drawing/size.ts';
import { type PlacedOn, type PlacementOptions } from '../../entities/placement.ts';
import type { Thing } from '../../entities/sets.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../../observation/presence.ts';
export type IconImage = string | ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement | OffscreenCanvas | ((g: OffscreenCanvasRenderingContext2D, size: number) => void);
export declare class IconAtlas {
    readonly names: string[];
    readonly size: number;
    cols: number;
    rows: number;
    failed: string[];
    version: number;
    constructor(images: Record<string, IconImage>, o?: {
        size?: number;
    });
    index(name: string): number;
    pixels(name: string): [number, number] | null;
    rect(name: string): [number, number];
    has(name: string, g?: Graphics): boolean;
    add(name: string, image: IconImage): this;
    onChange: (() => void) | null;
    load(loader: Loader): Promise<void>;
    get ready(): boolean;
    texture(g: Graphics): GTexture | null;
}
export declare const icons: (images: Record<string, IconImage>, o?: {
    size?: number;
}) => IconAtlas;
export interface IconProps<D = Thing> extends ElementProps, Omit<PlacementOptions<D>, 'heading'> {
    of: PlacedOn<D>;
    icons: IconAtlas;
    icon: string | ((d: D) => string | null | undefined);
    size?: SizeOf<number | readonly [number, number] | ((d: D) => number | readonly [number, number])>;
    anchor?: readonly [number, number] | ((d: D) => readonly [number, number]);
    feature?: {
        order?: (d: D) => number;
    };
    color?: Color | ((d: D) => Color);
    heading?: boolean | ((d: D) => number);
    tooltip?: (d: D, s: State) => string | null | undefined;
    onTap?: (f: Picked<D>, s: State) => void;
    onHover?: (f: Picked<D> | null, s: State) => void;
    select?: 'tap' | 'toggle' | false;
    pickable?: boolean;
}
export declare class Icon<D = Thing> extends Element<IconProps<D>> {
    readonly kind: 'point';
    constructor(p: IconProps<D>);
    attach(ctx: AttachContext): Promise<void>;
    setProps(given: Partial<Leveled<IconProps<D>>>): this;
    frame(ctx: FrameContext): DrawCall[];
    pickInfo(index: number): Omit<Picked, 'at' | 'x' | 'y'> | null;
    status(): ElementStatus;
    wantsFrames(_s: State): number;
    readouts(): {
        count: () => number;
    };
    detach(): void;
}
export declare function icon<D = Thing>(p: Leveled<IconProps<D>>): Icon<D>;

## core/encoding/marks/line.d.ts
import { type Feature } from '../../data/features.ts';
import type { FrameContext } from '../../world/element.ts';
import type { State } from '../../world/state.ts';
import type { DrawCall, Graphics } from '../../render/graphics/interface.ts';
import { type ParamBlock, type ShaderCode } from '../../render/graphics/shaders/hooks.ts';
import { type Color } from '../../render/style/color.ts';
import { Segments, SegmentsJob } from '../../geometry/build/segments.ts';
import { FeatureMark, Prog, type Entry, type FeatureMarkProps, type PerFeature, type Styled } from './features.ts';
import { type SizeOf, type SizeUnits } from '../../render/drawing/size.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../../observation/presence.ts';
export interface LineProps<D> extends FeatureMarkProps<D> {
    width?: SizeOf<PerFeature<number, D>>;
    color?: PerFeature<Color, D>;
    value?: PerFeature<number, D>;
    dashes?: PerFeature<readonly number[], D>;
    dashOffset?: PerFeature<number, D>;
    flow?: number;
    caps?: 'round' | 'butt' | 'square';
    joins?: 'bevel' | 'round' | 'miter';
    glow?: number;
    over?: boolean;
}
export declare const lineProgram: (g: Graphics, label: string, code: ShaderCode, params: ParamBlock, blend?: FeatureMarkProps<unknown>['blend'], over?: boolean) => Prog;
export declare class Line<D = Feature> extends FeatureMark<D, LineProps<D>> {
    readonly kind: 'line';
    protected readonly mark = "line";
    protected segs: Segments;
    protected drapeable(): boolean;
    protected drapeMark(): 'line';
    protected legacySits(): 'above' | undefined;
    get order(): number | undefined;
    animating(s: State): boolean;
    protected measuring(): boolean;
    wantsFrames(s: State): number;
    flowing(): boolean;
    protected selfMoving(): boolean;
    protected rangesStale(): boolean;
    protected programs(code: ShaderCode): Prog[];
    protected styleProps(): unknown[];
    protected sizeUnits(): SizeUnits;
    protected style(e: Entry<D>, s: State): Styled;
    static buildSliceMs: number;
    protected build(ctx: FrameContext): void | false;
    protected startBuild(next: Segments): SegmentsJob;
    protected alongOf(_e: Entry<D>, _k: number): ((at: number) => number) | undefined;
    protected built2(): void;
    protected upload(ctx: FrameContext): void;
    protected ground(ctx: FrameContext): void;
    protected uniforms(_ctx: FrameContext): void;
    protected draws(ctx: FrameContext): DrawCall[];
    protected preparePrograms(): void;
    protected graphicsBusy(): boolean;
    protected warm(): void;
    protected freeGeometry(): void;
    readouts(): {
        count: () => number;
        segments: () => number;
    };
}
export declare const line: <D = Feature>(p: Leveled<LineProps<D>>) => Line<D>;

## core/encoding/marks/points.d.ts
import type { FrameContext } from '../../world/element.ts';

export interface PointDatum {
    at: LonLatH;
    value?: number;
    id?: string | number;
    [k: string]: any;
}
export interface PointsProps<D = PointDatum> extends FeatureMarkProps<D> {
    at?: (d: D) => LonLatH;
    size?: SizeOf<PerFeature<number, D>>;
    color?: PerFeature<Color, D>;
    value?: PerFeature<number, D>;
    outline?: {
        color?: PerFeature<Color, D>;
        width?: PerFeature<number, D>;
    };
    along?: {
        times: (d: D) => readonly (number | Date)[] | null | undefined;
        clock?: (s: State) => number | Date;
    };
    behind?: number;
}

    readonly kind: 'point';

    protected style(e: Entry<D>, s: State): Styled;
    protected build(): void;
    protected ground(): void;
    protected uniforms(ctx: FrameContext): void;
    protected draws(ctx: FrameContext): DrawCall[];
    protected reordered(): void;
    animating(_s: State): boolean;
    status(): ReturnType<FeatureMark<D, PointsProps<D>>['status']>;
    protected freeGeometry(): void;
    readouts(): {
        count: () => number;
        shown: () => number;
    };
}

## core/encoding/marks/raster.d.ts
import { Element, type AttachContext, type FrameContext, type ElementProps, type ElementStatus } from '../../world/element.ts';
import type { DrawCall } from '../../render/graphics/interface.ts';
import { type ImageSource, type RasterBlend, type RasterSource, type ViewSource } from '../../data/sources/raster.ts';
import type { BBox } from '../../space/geo.ts';
import type { Feature } from '../../data/features.ts';
import type { Legend } from '../scales.ts';
import { Area, type AreaProps } from './area.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../../observation/presence.ts';
export interface RasterProps extends ElementProps {
    source: RasterSource;
    opacity?: number;
    blend?: RasterBlend;
}
export interface ImageRasterProps extends Omit<AreaProps<Feature>, 'data' | 'color' | 'height' | 'value'> {
    source: ImageSource | ViewSource;
    legend?: Legend;
}
export declare class Raster extends Element<RasterProps> {
    readonly kind: 'raster';
    constructor(p: RasterProps);
    painted(): RasterSource | null;
    frame(): DrawCall[];
    attribution(): string[];
    get fades(): boolean;
}
export declare class ImageRaster extends Area<Feature> {
    constructor(p: ImageRasterProps);
    protected drapeMark(): 'raster';
    protected imaged(): boolean;
    get picture(): {
        box: BBox | null;
        asked: number;
        loading: boolean;
    };
    attach(ctx: AttachContext): Promise<void>;
    frame(ctx: FrameContext): DrawCall[];
    status(): ElementStatus;
    detach(): void;
    attribution(): string[];
    get legendImage(): string | null;
}
declare function rasterOf(p: Leveled<RasterProps>): Raster;
declare function rasterOf(p: Leveled<ImageRasterProps>): ImageRaster;
export declare const raster: typeof rasterOf & {
    xyz(url: string | ((z: number, x: number, y: number) => string), o?: import("../../index.ts").XYZOptions): RasterSource;
    wms: {
        (url: string, o: import("../../data/sources/raster.ts").WMSOptions & {
            single: true;
        }): ViewSource;
        (url: string, o: import("../../data/sources/raster.ts").WMSOptions): RasterSource;
    } & {
        capabilities: (url: string, o?: import("../../index.ts").ServiceOptions & {
            version?: '1.3.0' | '1.1.1';
        }) => Promise<import("../../index.ts").WMSCapabilities>;
        legend: (url: string, layer: string, o?: {
            style?: string;
            version?: string;
            format?: string;
            proxy?: import("../../index.ts").ServiceOptions['proxy'];
        }) => string;
        info: (url: string, q: {
            layers: string;
            lon: number;
            lat: number;
            zoom?: number;
            metres?: number;
            count?: number;
            version?: '1.3.0' | '1.1.1';
            format?: string;
        }, o?: import("../../index.ts").ServiceOptions) => Promise<(Feature & {
            layer: string | null;
        })[]>;
    };
    view(o: import("../../data/sources/raster.ts").ViewSourceOptions & {
        url: (box: BBox, width: number, height: number) => string;
    }): ViewSource;
    field(o: import("../../data/sources/raster.ts").RasterSourceOptions & {
        value: (lon: number, lat: number) => number | null;
        ramp?: import("../../index.ts").Ramp;
        domain?: [number, number];
        cell?: number;
        alpha?: number;
    }): RasterSource;
    image(o: {
        url?: string;
        image?: ImageBitmap | OffscreenCanvas | HTMLCanvasElement;
        bounds: BBox;
        id?: string;
        attribution?: string;
    }): ImageSource;
    custom(o: import("../../data/sources/raster.ts").RasterSourceOptions & {
        draw: (g: import("../../data/sources/raster.ts").Ctx2D, tile: import("../../index.ts").RasterTile, ctx: import("../../index.ts").RasterContext) => boolean | Promise<boolean>;
    }): RasterSource;
};
export {};

## core/encoding/scales.d.ts
import type { State } from '../world/state.ts';
import { type Ramp } from '../render/style/ramp.ts';
import type { Feature } from '../data/features.ts';
export type Value<D> = (d: D, s?: State) => unknown;
export interface LegendItem {
    color?: string;
    size?: number;
    label: string;
    from?: number;
    to?: number;
    value?: unknown;
}
export interface Legend {
    kind: 'classes' | 'continuous' | 'categories' | 'image';
    items: LegendItem[];
    stops?: string[];
    domain?: [number, number];
    missing?: string;
    image?: string;
}
export interface Scale<D, T> {
    (d: any, s?: State): T;
    prepare(data: readonly D[], key?: string): void;
    readonly auto: boolean;
    legend(o?: {
        format?: (v: number) => string;
    }): Legend;
    readonly breaks: readonly unknown[];
    readonly kind: string;
    readonly version: number;
}
export interface ScaleOptions<T> {
    domain?: 'auto' | [number, number] | number[] | unknown[];
    range?: Ramp | [number, number] | T[];
    n?: number;
    clamp?: boolean;
    missing?: T;
    reverse?: boolean;
}
export declare function jenksBreaks(sorted: readonly number[], k: number): number[];
export declare function prettyBreaks(lo: number, hi: number, k: number): number[];
export declare const scale: {
    linear: <D = Feature, T = string>(value: Value<D>, o?: ScaleOptions<T>) => Scale<D, T>;
    log: <D = Feature, T = string>(value: Value<D>, o?: ScaleOptions<T>) => Scale<D, T>;
    sqrt: <D = Feature, T = string>(value: Value<D>, o?: ScaleOptions<T>) => Scale<D, T>;
    quantile: <D = Feature, T = string>(value: Value<D>, o?: ScaleOptions<T>) => Scale<D, T>;
    quantize: <D = Feature, T = string>(value: Value<D>, o?: ScaleOptions<T>) => Scale<D, T>;
    threshold: <D = Feature, T = string>(value: Value<D>, o?: ScaleOptions<T>) => Scale<D, T>;
    jenks: <D = Feature, T = string>(value: Value<D>, o?: ScaleOptions<T>) => Scale<D, T>;
    pretty: <D = Feature, T = string>(value: Value<D>, o?: ScaleOptions<T>) => Scale<D, T>;
    category: <D = Feature, T = string>(value: Value<D>, o?: ScaleOptions<T> & {
        map?: Record<string, T>;
        other?: T;
    }) => Scale<D, T>;
};
export declare const isScale: (v: unknown) => v is Scale<unknown, unknown>;

        q: Float32Array;

        q: Float32Array;
        p: Float32Array;

## core/entities/body/skin.d.ts
import { type Skeleton } from './skeleton.ts';
export declare const SKIN_FLOATS: number;
export declare const SKIN_INSTANCE_FLOATS: number;
export declare const SKIN_SLOTS: number;
export declare const rowTexels: (bones: number) => number;
export interface SkinMeshInput {
    positions: ArrayLike<number>;
    normals?: ArrayLike<number>;
    joints: ArrayLike<number>;
    weights: ArrayLike<number>;
    index: ArrayLike<number>;
    slots?: ArrayLike<number>;
    uvs?: ArrayLike<number>;
    morphs?: {
        a: ArrayLike<number>;
        b: ArrayLike<number>;
        pair: ArrayLike<number>;
    };
}
export interface SkinMesh {
    vertices: Float32Array;
    index: Uint32Array;
    bounds: [number, number, number, number, number, number];
}
export declare function skinMesh(o: SkinMeshInput): SkinMesh;
export declare function smoothNormals(P: ArrayLike<number>, index: ArrayLike<number>): Float32Array;
export interface PoseFrame {
    pose: Float32Array;
    root?: readonly number[];
    morphs?: readonly number[];
}
export interface Clip {
    frames: number;
    loop: boolean;
    data: Float32Array;
    distance?: number;
}
export declare function bakeClip(sk: Skeleton, frames: number, at: (t: number) => PoseFrame, o?: {
    loop?: boolean;
    distance?: number;
}): Clip;
export declare function writeRow(sk: Skeleton, fr: PoseFrame, out: Float32Array, at?: number): void;
export type Motion = {
    clip: string;
    at: number;
    to?: {
        clip: string;
        at: number;
        mix: number;
    };
} | {
    pose: PoseFrame;
    key?: unknown;
};
export interface Figure {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly mesh: SkinMesh;
    readonly skeleton: Skeleton;
    readonly clips: Readonly<Record<string, Clip>>;
    readonly material?: SkinMaterial | null;
    readonly height: number;
}
export interface SkinMaterial {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly wgsl: string;
    readonly glsl: string;
}
export declare function figure(o: {
    id: string;
    mesh: SkinMesh;
    skeleton: Skeleton;
    clips?: Record<string, Clip>;
    material?: SkinMaterial | null;
    height?: number;
}): Figure;

## core/entities/index.d.ts
import type { World } from '../world/world.ts';
import { Solids, type SolidsProps } from '../geometry/shapes/solids.ts';
import { INSTANCE_FLOATS } from '../geometry/shapes/mesh.ts';
import type { Made } from '../geometry/shapes/shape.ts';
import type { Network } from '../geometry/networks/index.ts';
import { type Figure, type Motion } from './body/skin.ts';
import { dataDrive } from './sets.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../observation/presence.ts';
type LonLat = [number, number];
export interface Want {
    forward: number;
    right: number;
    up: number;
    turn: number;
    fast: boolean;
    jump: boolean;
    to?: LonLat;
}
export declare const still: () => Want;
export interface Locomotion {
    readonly kind: 'none' | 'surface' | 'air';
    step(e: Entity, want: Want, dt: number, w: GroundWorld, look: number): void;
}
export interface GroundWorld {
    heightAt(lon: number, lat: number): number;
    readonly exaggeration: number;
    standAt?(lon: number, lat: number, near?: number): number;
    readonly standVersion?: number;
}
export declare const none: () => Locomotion;
export declare function surface(o?: {
    on?: 'walkable' | Network | LonLat[];
    speed?: number;
    run?: number;
    blocked?: (lon: number, lat: number) => number;
    jump?: number;
    wheels?: {
        top?: number;
        boost?: number;
    };
}): Locomotion;
export declare function air(o?: {
    ceiling?: number;
    speed?: number;
    fast?: number;
    hover?: boolean;
    glide?: number;
    over?: 'ground' | 'sea';
}): Locomotion;
export declare const locomotion: {
    none: () => Locomotion;
    surface: (o?: Parameters<typeof surface>[0]) => Locomotion;
    air: (o?: Parameters<typeof air>[0]) => Locomotion;
};
export declare const groundOf: (w: GroundWorld, lon: number, lat: number, near?: number) => number;
export interface InputState {
    keys: Set<string>;
    stick: [number, number];
    up: number;
}
export interface Controller {
    readonly kind: 'walk' | 'ride' | 'fly' | 'custom';
    read(i: InputState): Want;
    readonly help: string;
}
export declare const controllers: {
    walk(): Controller;
    ride(): Controller;
    fly(): Controller;
    custom(read: (i: InputState) => Partial<Want>, help?: string): Controller;
    orbit(o?: CameraControlOptions): CameraController;
    pan(o?: Omit<CameraControlOptions, 'rotate' | 'tilt' | 'drag'>): CameraController;
};
export interface CameraControlOptions {
    pan?: boolean;
    rotate?: boolean;
    tilt?: boolean;
    zoom?: boolean | {
        wheel?: boolean;
        pinch?: boolean;
        keys?: boolean;
    };
     zoomRange?: [number, number];
     tiltRange?: [number, number];
     drag?: 'pan' | 'orbit';
    bounds?: [number, number, number, number];
    viscosity?: number;
    snap?: number;
    step?: number;
    keys?: boolean;
}
export interface CameraController {
    readonly kind: 'orbit' | 'pan';
    readonly help: string;
    readonly gestures: {
        pan: boolean;
        rotate: boolean;
        tilt: boolean;
        zoom: boolean;
    };
     readonly zoomInputs?: {
        wheel: boolean;
        pinch: boolean;
        keys: boolean;
    };
    readonly zoomRange?: [number, number];
    readonly tiltRange?: [number, number];
    readonly drag?: 'pan' | 'orbit';
    readonly bounds?: [number, number, number, number];
    readonly viscosity?: number;
    readonly snap?: number;
    readonly step?: number;
    readonly keys?: boolean;
}
export interface Drive {
    readonly kind: 'player' | 'script' | 'none';
    want(e: Entity, input: InputState, dt: number): Want;
}

    player(c: Controller): Drive & {
        controller: Controller;
    };
    script(fn: (e: Entity, dt: number) => Partial<Want>): Drive;
    data: typeof dataDrive;
};

import type { Anchor, Part, V3 } from '../geometry/shapes/parts.ts';
export type Where = 'ground' | 'front' | 'outside' | 'inside' | 'wall' | 'up';
export type Measure = 'footprint' | 'front' | 'roof';
export interface TraitSpec {
    about?: string;
    anchors?: string;
    parts?: string;
    measure?: Measure;
    param?: string;
    guess?: (m: MadeLike) => unknown;
    data?: string;
    def?: unknown;
    min?: number;
    max?: number;
    per?: string;
    where?: Where | readonly Where[];
    optional?: boolean;
}
export interface MadeLike {
    levels: readonly unknown[];
    anchors: readonly Anchor[];
    parts: readonly Part[];
    bounds: [V3, V3];
    groups?: readonly {
        name: string;
        first: number;
        count: number;
    }[];
    params?: Record<string, unknown>;
    kind?: string;
}

    about?: string;
    traits: Record<string, TraitSpec>;
    extends?: EntityKind;
}

## core/geometry/networks/bake-types.d.ts
import type { P2, RoadEdge } from './graph.ts';
import type { PathsD } from 'clipper2-ts';
import type { Ground } from '../landform/compose/ground.ts';
import type { StageGate, PatchOptions } from '../../runtime/workers/compile/patch-policy.ts';
import type { WalkEdge, WalkReport } from './walk-bake.ts';
export type { Ground } from '../landform/compose/ground.ts';
export type P3 = [number, number, number];
export type Piece = {
    t: 'strip';
    kind: string;
    edges: P3[][];
    info: number;
    overhead?: boolean;
} | {
    t: 'poly';
    kind: string;
    ring: P3[];
    fan?: boolean;
    info: number;
} | {
    t: 'wall';
    kind: string;
    line: [number, number, number, number][];
    info: number;
} | {
    t: 'box';
    kind: string;
    at: P3;
    top: number;
    w: number;
    d: number;
    turn: number;
    info: number;
}
 | {
    t: 'area';
    kind: string;
    rings: P3[][];
    down?: boolean;
    info: number;
    overhead?: boolean;
    level?: number;
}
 | {
    t: 'edge';
    kind: string;
    pts: [number, number, number, number, number, number][];
    info: number;
};
export interface ClampOut {
    cells: P3[][][];
    edges: P3[][];
    terrain?: Ground;
}
export interface SolveOptions extends PatchOptions {
    place?: string;
    snapshots?: boolean;
    verges?: Record<number, number>;
    pruneDanglingStubs?: boolean;
    simplifyShortSegments?: boolean;
    relaxHairpins?: boolean;
    smoothAlignment?: boolean;
    banking?: boolean;
    minK?: number;
    verticalFloors?: {
        road: string;
        at: P2;
        s?: number;
        minimum: number;
    }[];
    repairPass?: number;
    surfaceLevels?: Record<string, number>;
    widenLowerRoads?: boolean;
}
export interface SolveReport {
    stages: StageGate[];
    repairs?: {
        stage: number;
        flagged: (string | number)[];
        counts: Record<string, number>;
        worst?: Record<string, number>;
        locations?: StageGate["locations"];
    }[];
    roads: number;
    levels: number;
    bridges: number;
    tunnels: number;
    portals: number;
    lifted: number;
    zones: number;
    planar: number;
    nonPlanarAt: P2[];
    islands: number;
    holes: number;
    invalid: number;
    slivers: number;
    sliverAt: P2[];

    }[];

        }[];
    };
}

import type { Feature } from '../../data/features.ts';

     a: number;
    b: number;

    dist: Float64Array;
    prev: Int32Array;
    origin: Int32Array;

        max?: number;
    }): number;
    pathFrom(at: LonLat, o?: {
        max?: number;
    }): LonLat[] | null;
}

    readonly xs: number[];
    readonly zs: number[];

    readonly radj: number[][];

    readonly pieceWay: number[];

    constructor(o: NetworkOptions<P>);
    get size(): {
        nodes: number;
        pieces: number;
    };
    local(p: LonLat): [number, number];
    lonLat(x: number, z: number): LonLat;
    nearest(at: LonLat, o?: {
        max?: number;
    }): Nearest | null;
    shortest(sources: readonly ({
        at: LonLat;
        cost?: number;
    } | {
        node: number;
        cost?: number;
    })[], o?: {
        max?: number;
        toward?: boolean;
    }): Shortest;
    route(from: LonLat, to: LonLat, o?: {
        max?: number;
    }): {
        cost: number;
        line: LonLat[];
    } | null;
    matrix(from: readonly LonLat[], to: readonly LonLat[], o?: {
        max?: number;
    }): number[][];

        at: LonLat;
        cost?: number;
    } | {
        node: number;
        cost?: number;
    })[] | Shortest, within: readonly number[], o?: {
        max?: number;
        cell?: number;
        reach?: number;
        off?: number;
    }): Feature[];
}

import type { StageGate } from '../../runtime/workers/compile/patch-policy.ts';

type P2 = [number, number];

    pts: P2[];

}
export declare const SLIVER: number, MIN_AREA: number, GAP: number;

    outer: PathD;

}

}
export interface Run {
    baseLevel: number;
    s0: number;
    chain: Chain;
    ci: number;
    level: number;
    bridge: boolean;
    pts: P2[];
    hs: number[];
    banks: number[];
    acc: number[];
    half: number;
    outer: number;
    nodes: [number | null, number | null];
    caps: [Cap | null, Cap | null];

    next: Run | null;

    t: P2;

    level: number;
}
export interface SurfaceInput {
    snapshot?: (stage: number, pieces?: Piece[], terrain?: Ground) => void;

        ring: P2[];
        half: number;
        level: number;
    }[];

    reserves?: P2[][];
    footpathWidth?: (x: number, z: number) => number;
    waters?: readonly SolvedWater[];
    options?: SolveOptions;
    terrain: Ground;
    gate: (gate: StageGate) => void;
    patch: (stage: number, change: Patch, infoIds?: number[]) => void;
}
export declare function surfaces(I: SurfaceInput, pieces: Piece[], clamp: ClampOut, report: SolveReport): {

};

    x: number;
    z: number;

    tz: number;
};

    d: number;
    dist: number;
};

## core/geometry/shapes/builder.d.ts
import { type Colorish } from './mesh.ts';
import { Parts, type V2, type V3 } from './parts.ts';
import { Scope } from './scope.ts';
import { type LampKind, type Material, type MaterialName as AnyMaterialName } from '../../render/look/materials.ts';
export type { V2, V3 };
export declare const MATERIALS_LOOK: {
    readonly matte: 0;
    readonly painted: 1;
    readonly glass: 2;
    readonly metal: 3;
    readonly emissive: 4;
    readonly foliage: 5;
    readonly water: 6;
};
export type MaterialName = AnyMaterialName;
export interface Finish {
    color?: Colorish;
    material?: MaterialName | Material;
    round?: number;
    detail?: 0 | 1 | 2;
    tint?: boolean;
    lamps?: LampKind;
}
export interface LabelOpts extends Finish {
    at?: V3;
    facing?: V3;
    along?: V3;
    height?: number;
    off?: number;
}
export type SolidLabel = string | {
    text: string;
    on?: 'front' | 'back' | 'left' | 'right' | 'top';
    at?: V2;
    height?: number;
    color?: Colorish;
    material?: MaterialName | Material;
    lamps?: LampKind;
};
export type SolidKind = 'box' | 'cylinder' | 'sphere' | 'cone' | 'capsule';
export interface SolidOpts extends Finish {
    size?: number | V3;
    radius?: number;
    top?: number;
    at?: V3;
    axis?: 'x' | 'y' | 'z';
    turn?: number;
    sides?: number;
    label?: SolidLabel;
}
export type Profile = readonly V2[] | {
    circle: number;
    sides?: number;
} | {
    rect: V2;
};
export type Path = readonly V3[] | {
    up: number;
} | {
    around: 'y';
    segments?: number;
    angle?: number;
};
export interface SweepOpts extends Finish {
    caps?: boolean;
    scale?: number | V2;
    twist?: number;
    smooth?: boolean;
}
export type CopyHow = {
    mirror: 'x' | 'z';
    keep?: boolean;
} | {
    repeat: number;
    step?: V3;
    turn?: number;
    axis?: 'x' | 'y' | 'z' | V3;
    at?: V3;
} | {
    along: readonly V3[];
    every: number;
    align?: boolean;
} | {
    scatter: readonly V2[];
    count: number;
    seed?: number;
    turn?: boolean;
} | {
    emit: EmitSpec;
};
export interface EmitSpec {
    count: number | readonly number[];
    life?: number;
    rise?: number;
    spread?: number;
    grow?: number;
    fade?: readonly [number, number];
    drift?: readonly [number, number];
}
export declare class Builder extends Parts {
    tintColor: Colorish | null;
    get triangles(): number;
    solid(kind: SolidKind, o?: SolidOpts): this;
    label(text: string, o?: LabelOpts): this;
    extrude(footprint: readonly V2[], height: number, o?: SweepOpts): this;
    lathe(profile: readonly V2[], o?: SweepOpts & {
        segments?: number;
        angle?: number;
    }): this;
    sweep(profile: Profile, path: Path, o?: SweepOpts): this;
    copy(fn: (b: Builder, i: number) => void, how: CopyHow): this;
    scope(box: {
        size: V3;
        at?: V3;
        turn?: number;
    } | {
        footprint: readonly V2[];
        height: number;
    }): Scope;
    mesh(points: readonly V3[], o?: Finish & {
        smooth?: boolean;
        crease?: number;
        lines?: boolean;
    }): this;
    tri(a: V3, b: V3, c: V3, n: [V3, V3, V3] | null, o: Finish): void;
}

## core/geometry/shapes/instances.d.ts
import type { AttachContext } from '../../world/element.ts';
import { Source } from '../../data/sources/source.ts';
import type { Feature } from '../../data/features.ts';
import { Solids, type SolidsProps } from './solids.ts';
import type { Made, Shape } from './shape.ts';
import type { SizeRange } from '../../render/drawing/size.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../../observation/presence.ts';
export interface EntitiesProps<P = Record<string, unknown>> extends Omit<SolidsProps, 'params'> {
    shape: Shape<never, never> | Made | {
        make(p?: Partial<P>, seed?: number): {
            levels: readonly unknown[];
            bounds: unknown;
        };
        id: string;
    };
    data: Source | Feature[];
    at?: (d: Feature) => [number, number] | [number, number, number];
    params?: (d: Feature) => Partial<P>;
    heading?: (d: Feature) => number;
    scale?: (d: Feature) => number;
    tint?: (d: Feature) => string | number;
    size?: SizeRange;
    stretch?: (d: Feature) => number;
    tints?: Record<string, (d: Feature) => string | number | undefined>;
    joints?: Record<string, JointDriver | ((d: Feature) => JointDriver)>;
    attach?: {
        shape: {
            make(p?: Record<string, unknown>, seed?: number): Made;
        };
        at: string;
        params?: Record<string, unknown>;
    }[];
    variation?: {
        seed?: (d: Feature) => number | string;
        color?: number;
        scale?: number;
        builds?: number;
    };
}
export type JointDriver = {
    rate: number;
    unit?: 'rpm' | 'deg/s' | 'rad/s' | 'm/s';
    phase?: number;
} | {
    formula: string;
} | {
    value: string;
    per?: number;
} | null;
declare const UNIT: {
    readonly rpm: number;
    readonly 'deg/s': number;
    readonly 'rad/s': 1;
    readonly 'm/s': 1;
};
export declare const rateOf: (d: {
    rate: number;
    unit?: keyof typeof UNIT;
}, slider: boolean) => number;
export declare class Entities<P = Record<string, unknown>> extends Solids {

    detach(): void;
    refresh(): this;
    joint(id: string | number, name: string, d: JointDriver): this;
    joint(name: string, d: JointDriver): this;
}
export declare const entities: <P = Record<string, unknown>>(p: Leveled<EntitiesProps<P>>) => Entities<P>;

## core/geometry/shapes/scope.d.ts
import { type V2, type V3 } from './parts.ts';
import type { Builder, Finish, SolidKind, SolidOpts } from './builder.ts';
import { type RoofFaceOptions, type RoofForm } from './roofs.ts';
import { Shape, type ParamSpec, type ParamsOf, type ShapeSpec } from './shape.ts';
export declare class Scope {
    readonly b: Builder;
    readonly o: V3;
    readonly x: V3;
    readonly y: V3;
    readonly z: V3;
    readonly size: V3;
    constructor(b: Builder, o: V3, x: V3, y: V3, z: V3, size: V3, ring?: readonly V2[] | null);
    static footprint(b: Builder, ring: readonly V2[], height: number): Scope;
    get width(): number;
    get height(): number;
    get depth(): number;
    at(u: number, v: number, w: number, metres?: boolean): V3;
    split(axis: 'x' | 'y' | 'z', sizes: readonly (number | '*' | `~${number}`)[]): Scope[];
    repeat(axis: 'x' | 'y' | 'z', size: number): Scope[];
    faces(): {
        front: Scope;
        back: Scope;
        left: Scope;
        right: Scope;
        top: Scope;
        bottom: Scope;
        walls: Scope[];
    };
    get footprint(): V2[];
    solid(kind?: SolidKind, o?: Omit<SolidOpts, 'size' | 'at' | 'turn'> & {
        inset?: number;
    }): this;
    panel(o?: Finish & {
        depth?: number;
        out?: number;
        inset?: number;
        flat?: boolean;
    }): this;
    build(fn: (b: Builder, s: {
        width: number;
        height: number;
        depth: number;
    }) => void): this;
    roof(kind: RoofForm, o?: Finish & {
        pitch?: number;
        overhang?: number;
        ridge?: RoofFaceOptions['ridge'];
        rise?: number;
        gablet?: number;
        high?: RoofFaceOptions['high'];
        ends?: Finish;
    }): this;
    extrude(depth: number): Scope;
    inset(left: number, bottom: number, right?: number, top?: number): Scope;
}
export interface GrammarSpec<D extends Record<string, ParamSpec>, X> {
    id?: string;
    params?: D;
    start?: string;
    rules: Record<string, (s: Scope, p: ParamsOf<D> & X, go: (rule: string, s: Scope) => void, rand: () => number) => void>;
    lot: (p: ParamsOf<D> & X) => {
        footprint: readonly V2[];
        height: number;
    } | {
        size: V3;
        at?: V3;
        turn?: number;
    };
    far?: ShapeSpec<D, X>['far'];
    budget?: ShapeSpec<D, X>['budget'];
}
export declare function grammar<D extends Record<string, ParamSpec> = Record<string, never>, X = object>(g: GrammarSpec<D, X>): Shape<D, X>;

## core/geometry/shapes/shape.d.ts
import { MeshBuilder, type Colorish } from './mesh.ts';
import { Builder } from './builder.ts';
import type { Anchor, Emitter, Part, V3 } from './parts.ts';
import { type ImpostorLevel } from './levels.ts';
import { type EntityKind } from '../../entities/kinds.ts';
import type { SizeRange } from '../../render/drawing/size.ts';
export type ParamSpec = {
    kind: 'num';
    min: number;
    max: number;
    def: number;
} | {
    kind: 'int';
    min: number;
    max: number;
    def: number;
} | {
    kind: 'choice';
    options: readonly string[];
    def: string;
} | {
    kind: 'color';
    def: string;
} | {
    kind: 'flag';
    def: boolean;
} | {
    kind: 'text';
    def: string;
    max: number;
};
export declare const num: (min: number, max: number, def?: number) => {
    kind: 'num';
    min: number;
    max: number;
    def: number;
};
export declare const int: (min: number, max: number, def?: number) => {
    kind: 'int';
    min: number;
    max: number;
    def: number;
};
export declare const choice: (options: readonly string[], def?: string) => {
    kind: 'choice';
    options: readonly string[];
    def: string;
};
export declare const color: (def: string) => {
    kind: 'color';
    def: string;
};
export declare const flag: (def?: boolean) => {
    kind: 'flag';
    def: boolean;
};
export declare const text: (def?: string, max?: number) => {
    kind: 'text';
    def: string;
    max: number;
};
type ParamValue<S extends ParamSpec> = S extends {
    kind: 'num' | 'int';
} ? number : S extends {
    kind: 'flag';
} ? boolean : string;
export type ParamsOf<D extends Record<string, ParamSpec>> = {
    [K in keyof D]: ParamValue<D[K]>;
};
export interface ShapeSpec<D extends Record<string, ParamSpec>, X = object> {
    id?: string;
    params?: D;
    defaults?: (given: Partial<ParamsOf<D>>) => Partial<ParamsOf<D>> | null | undefined;
    build: (b: Builder, p: ParamsOf<D> & X, rand: () => number) => void;
    simple?: boolean | ((b: Builder, p: ParamsOf<D> & X, rand: () => number) => void);
    far?: ((b: Builder, p: ParamsOf<D> & X, rand: () => number) => void) | 'impostor' | 'box' | 'dot' | 'none';
    size?: SizeRange;
    budget?: {
        whole?: number;
        simple?: number;
        far?: number;
    };
    kind?: EntityKind | string;
}
export interface Made {
    levels: readonly (Float32Array | ImpostorLevel | null)[];
    parts: Part[];
    anchors: Anchor[];
    triangles: number[];
    bounds: [V3, V3];
    groups?: {
        name: string;
        first: number;
        count: number;
    }[];
    pieces?: Pieces;
    params?: Record<string, unknown>;
    kind?: string;
    size?: SizeRange;
}
export interface ShapeCheck {
    ok: boolean;
    problems: string[];
    triangles: {
        whole: number;
        simple: number;
        far: number;
    };
    builds: number;
}
export declare class Shape<D extends Record<string, ParamSpec> = Record<string, ParamSpec>, X = object> {

    make(p?: Partial<ParamsOf<D> & X>, seed?: number): Made;
    into(mb: MeshBuilder, p: Partial<ParamsOf<D> & X>, o?: {
        at?: V3;
        turn?: number;
        id?: number;
        level?: number;
        seed?: number;
        anchor?: [number, number];

    }): Builder;

        kind?: EntityKind | string;
    }): ShapeCheck;

export declare const CACHE_ENTRIES: number, CACHE_MB: number;

## core/observation/observers/orbit/aim.d.ts
import { type LonLatH } from '../../../space/geo.ts';
import type { Pose } from './views.ts';
export interface Subject {
    lon: number;
    lat: number;
    up?: number;
    heading?: number;
}
export interface Track {
    readonly track: {
        at(id: string, t: number): {
            lon: number;
            lat: number;
            heading: number;
            speed?: number;
        } | null;
    };
    readonly id: string;
    readonly up?: number | ((lon: number, lat: number) => number);
}
export type Target = LonLatH | Subject | string | Track | ((timeMs: number) => LonLatH | Subject | null | undefined);
export declare function track(log: Track['track'], id: string, o?: {
    up?: number | ((lon: number, lat: number) => number);
}): Track;
export interface Aim {
    readonly kind: 'look-at';
    readonly target: Target;
    readonly lead?: number;
    readonly raise?: number;
}
export declare function lookAt(target: Target, o?: {
    lead?: number;
    raise?: number;
}): Aim;
export declare function checkTarget(t: Target, where: string): void;
export interface Ground {
    heightAt(lon: number, lat: number): number;
    readonly exaggeration?: number;
    pointAt?(name: string): LonLatH | null;
}
export interface Where {
    lon: number;
    lat: number;
    y: number;
    g: number;
    heading: number;
}
export declare function whereOf(t: Target, g: Ground, timeMs: number): Where | null;
export interface Rig {
    dist: number;
    tilt: number;
    bearing: number;
    raise?: number;
}
export declare const ahead: (lon: number, lat: number, brg: number, m: number) => [number, number];
export declare function rigPose(P: {
    lon: number;
    lat: number;
    y: number;
}, r: Rig, ground: (lon: number, lat: number) => number, fov: number, floor?: (h: number) => number): {
    pose: Pose;
    ground: number;
};
export declare function rigFrom(E: {
    lon: number;
    lat: number;
    y: number;
}, P: {
    lon: number;
    lat: number;
    y: number;
}, raise?: number): Rig;
export declare function eyeOf(P: {
    lon: number;
    lat: number;
    y: number;
}, r: Rig): {
    lon: number;
    lat: number;
    y: number;
};
export declare function eyeOfPose(p: Pose, groundY: number): {
    lon: number;
    lat: number;
    y: number;
};
