    jump(v: View | Pose): void;
    place(p: Pose, groundY?: number): void;
    framed(p: Pose): Pose;
    fly(to: View, o?: FlyOptions): Promise<void>;
    play(k: Keys, o?: {
        loop?: boolean;
    }): Promise<void>;
    get moving(): boolean;
    stop(): void;
    drive(step: (dt: number) => boolean | void): Promise<void>;
    get driving(): boolean;
    update(w: number, h: number, dtMs: number): void;
    setProjection(p: 'auto' | 'globe' | 'flat', seconds?: number): void;
    morphAt(dist: number): number;
    clearance: number;
    clear: number | false;
    under: ((lon: number, lat: number, y: number) => number) | null;
    planes(dist?: number): [number, number];
    ray(px: number, py: number): {
        o: [number, number, number];
        d: [number, number, number];
    };
    groundHit(px: number, py: number, h?: number): [number, number, number] | null;
    lonLatAt(px: number, py: number): [number, number] | null;
    projectWorld(x: number, y: number, z: number): [number, number] | null;
    project(lon: number, lat: number, h?: number): [number, number] | null;
    facing(lon: number, lat: number, h?: number): boolean;
    zoomBy(f: number): void;
    zoomAt(f: number, px: number, py: number): void;
    pan(dx: number, dy: number): void;
    moveBy(ex: number, ez: number): void;
    turn(dYawRad: number, dPitchRad: number): void;
    key(k: string): boolean;
    focusable(): void;
    attach(el: HTMLElement, onTap?: (x: number, y: number) => void): () => void;
}

## core/observation/observers/orbit/moves.d.ts
import { type Ease } from './ease.ts';

import type { Pose } from './views.ts';
export interface RigSpec {
    dist?: number;
    tilt?: number;
    bearing?: number;
    turn?: number;
    raise?: number;
}

    at: number;
    rig: RigSpec | 'now';
    ease?: Ease;
}
export interface Move {
    readonly kind: string;
    readonly aim: Aim;
    readonly keys: readonly RigKey[];
    readonly damping?: Damping;
    readonly over?: number | false;
    readonly seconds?: number;
}
export declare function move(m: {
    kind?: string;
    aim: Aim | Target;
    keys: readonly RigKey[];
    damping?: Damping;
    over?: number | false;
    seconds?: number;
}): Move;
interface Common {
    damping?: Damping;
    over?: number | false;
    raise?: number;
    ease?: Ease;
    seconds?: number;
}
export declare const moves: {
    chase(t: Aim | Target, o?: Common & {
        behind?: number;
        up?: number;
        turn?: number;
        to?: RigSpec;
    }): Move;
    track(t: Aim | Target, o?: Common & {
        side?: number;
        up?: number;
        to?: RigSpec;
    }): Move;
    crane(t: Aim | Target, o?: Common & {
        up?: number;
        back?: number;
        swing?: number;
        to?: RigSpec;
    }): Move;
    dolly(t: Aim | Target, o?: Common & {
        in?: number;
        out?: number;
        to?: number;
    }): Move;
    arc(t: Aim | Target, o?: Common & {
        degrees?: number;
        dist?: number;
        tilt?: number;
        from?: RigSpec;
    }): Move;
    reveal(t: Aim | Target, o?: Common & {
        from?: 'below' | 'behind' | 'side';
        dist?: number;
        to?: RigSpec;
    }): Move;
};
export interface MoveWorld extends Ground {
    readonly timeMs: number;
    topIn?(w: number, s: number, e: number, n: number): number | null;
}
export declare class Shot {

    readonly seconds: number | undefined;
    readonly follower: Follower;

        seconds?: number;
        ease?: Ease;
    }, follower?: Follower);
    progress(t?: number): number;
    get done(): boolean;
    step(dt: number, w: MoveWorld, cam: {
        pose: Pose;
        groundY: number;
        fov: number;
        floor: (h: number) => number;
    }): {
        pose: Pose;
        ground: number;
    } | null;
    poseAt(t: number, w: MoveWorld, cam: {
        pose: Pose;
        groundY: number;
        fov: number;
        floor: (h: number) => number;
    }, timeMs?: number): Pose | null;

}
export type { Place };

import type { Api, Quality, State } from '../world/state.ts';

export type Range = readonly [number, number];
export interface WhenShorthand {
    level?: LevelSpec;
    hour?: Range;
    year?: Range;
    month?: Range;
    api?: Api | Api[];
    quality?: Quality;
    role?: string | string[];
    toggle?: string;
}
export type WhenFn = (s: State) => boolean | number;
export type When = boolean | WhenShorthand | WhenFn;
export declare function shown(when: When | undefined, s: State, where?: string): number;
export declare function tracked<T extends object>(o: T, rec: Set<string>, path?: string): T;
export declare function readsOf(s: object, paths: readonly string[]): string;

export declare function asksLevel(when: When | undefined, s: State): boolean;
export interface LevelValues<T> {
    level: Record<string, T>;
}
export type ByLevel<T> = T | LevelValues<T>;
export type Leveled<P> = {
    [K in keyof P]: P[K] | LevelValues<P[K]>;
};
export declare function isByLevel(v: unknown): v is LevelValues<unknown>;

## core/render/graphics/interface.d.ts
export type Api = 'webgpu' | 'webgl2';
export interface Caps {
    api: Api;
    compute: boolean;
    timer: boolean;
    floatTargets: boolean;
    maxTex: number;
    maxLayers: number;
    vertexTextures: number;
    f16: boolean;
    device: string;
}
export type TexFormat = 'rgba8' | 'rgba32f' | 'r32f';
export interface TextureDesc {
    label?: string;
    width: number;
    height: number;
    layers?: number;
    format: TexFormat;
    filter?: 'linear' | 'nearest';
    renderable?: boolean;
    growable?: boolean;
}
export interface GTexture {
    readonly desc: TextureDesc;
    write(data: ArrayBufferView, o?: {
        x?: number;
        y?: number;
        layer?: number;
        width?: number;
        height?: number;
    }): void;
    writeImage(src: ImageBitmap | OffscreenCanvas | HTMLCanvasElement, o?: {
        x?: number;
        y?: number;
        layer?: number;
        premultiplied?: boolean;
    }): void;
    destroy(): void;
    readonly version?: number;
}
export type BufferKind = 'uniform' | 'index' | 'instance' | 'vertex';
export interface GBuffer {
    readonly kind: BufferKind;
    readonly size: number;
    write(data: ArrayBufferView, offsetBytes?: number): void;
    destroy(): void;
    readonly uid: number;
    readonly version: number;
}
export interface Attribute {
    name: string;
    location: number;
    size: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
    offset: number;
}
export interface ProgramDesc {
    label: string;
    wgsl: string;
    glsl: {
        vertex: string;
        fragment: string;
    };
    blocks: string[];
    textures?: {
        name: string;
        kind: '2d' | '2d-array';
        sample: 'float' | 'unfilterable';
    }[];
    instance?: {
        stride: number;
        attributes: Attribute[];
    };
    vertex?: {
        stride: number;
        attributes: Attribute[];
    };
    blend?: 'none' | 'alpha' | 'add' | 'premultiplied' | 'multiply' | 'screen';
    depth?: {
        write: boolean;
        test: 'less' | 'less-equal' | 'greater-equal' | 'always';
    };
    cull?: 'none' | 'back' | 'front';
    topology?: 'triangles' | 'lines';
    sceneDepth?: boolean;
    groundDepth?: boolean;
    overlay?: boolean;
    format?: TexFormat;
}
export interface Program {
    readonly label: string;
    readonly desc: ProgramDesc;
    readonly ready: Promise<void>;
    destroy(): void;
}
export interface DrawCall {
    program: Program;
    blocks: GBuffer[];
    textures?: GTexture[];
    instances?: GBuffer;
    vertices?: GBuffer;
    index?: GBuffer;
    count: number;
    instanceCount?: number;
    pickProgram?: () => Program;
    ground?: GroundDraw;
    first?: number;
    cast?: boolean;
    bounds?: readonly number[];
    shadowOnly?: boolean;
    upper?: boolean | 'ground';
    stands?: boolean;
    keepDepth?: boolean;
}
export interface GroundDraw {
    version: string;
    since?: {
        version: string;
        boxes: ArrayLike<number>;
    };
    ready: boolean;
    where?: readonly [number, number, number, number] | null;
    tile(t: {
        key: number;
        bounds: [number, number, number, number];
        box: [number, number, number, number];
        block: GBuffer;
    }, out: DrawCall[]): void;
}
export interface GTarget {
    readonly width: number;
    readonly height: number;
    readonly texture?: GTexture;
    destroy(): void;
}
export interface FrameOptions {
    clear: [number, number, number, number];
    read?: boolean;
    target?: GTarget;
    scissor?: [number, number, number, number];
    keep?: boolean;
    keepDepth?: boolean;
    aa?: boolean;
}
export interface Graphics {
    flush?(): void;
    hold?(on: boolean): void;
    readonly api: Api;
    readonly caps: Caps;
    readonly width: number;
    readonly height: number;
    resize(width: number, height: number): void;
    buffer(kind: BufferKind, sizeOrData: number | ArrayBufferView, label?: string): GBuffer;
    texture(desc: TextureDesc): GTexture;
    target(width: number, height: number, o?: {
        sampled?: boolean;
        label?: string;
    }): GTarget;
    layerTarget(texture: GTexture, layer: number): GTarget;
    copyLayers(from: GTexture, to: GTexture, layers: number): boolean;
    readTexture(texture: GTexture): Promise<Float32Array | Uint8Array>;
    program(desc: ProgramDesc): Program;
    frame(draws: DrawCall[], o: FrameOptions): Promise<Uint8Array> | null;
    readonly stats: {
        draws: number;
        passes: number;
        uploadBytes: number;
        uploads: number;
        gpuMs: number | null;
        uploadMaxMs: number;
    };
    readonly lost: Promise<string>;
    readonly bytes: number;
    memory(): import('./live.ts').HeldItem[];
    destroy(): void;
}
export declare const FRAME_FLOATS: number;

        };
        color: {
            type: string;
            doc: string;
            mutable: boolean;
        };
        time: {
            type: string;
            doc: string;
            mutable: boolean;
        };
        eye: {
            type: string;
            doc: string;
            mutable: boolean;
        };
        origin: {
            type: string;
            doc: string;
            mutable: boolean;
        };
    };
    fragment: {
        color: {
            type: string;
            doc: string;
            mutable: boolean;
        };
        corner: {
            type: string;
            doc: string;
            mutable: boolean;
        };
        uv: {
            type: string;
            doc: string;
            mutable: boolean;
        };
        normal: {
            type: string;
            doc: string;
            mutable: boolean;
        };
        position: {
            type: string;
            doc: string;
            mutable: boolean;
        };
        time: {
            type: string;
            doc: string;
            mutable: boolean;
        };
    };
};
export interface MeshCode {
    vertexAttributes: Record<string, string>;
    instanceAttributes: Record<string, string>;
    varyings: Record<string, string>;
    textures: string[];
    vertex?: string;
    fragment?: string;
    functions?: string;
}
export declare function assembleCustomMesh(base: Sources, code: MeshCode, params: ParamBlock, where: string): Sources & {
    vertexAttrs: {
        name: string;
        size: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
    }[];
    instanceAttrs: {
        name: string;
        size: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
    }[];
};

## core/render/graphics/shaders/translate.d.ts
import { AslnutError } from '../../../world/errors.ts';
export type Scalar = 'f32' | 'i32' | 'u32' | 'bool';
export type Type = {
    k: 'scalar';
    s: Scalar;
    abstract?: boolean;
} | {
    k: 'vec';
    n: 2 | 3 | 4;
    s: Scalar;
} | {
    k: 'mat';
    c: 2 | 3 | 4;
    r: 2 | 3 | 4;
} | {
    k: 'array';
    of: Type;
    n: number;
} | {
    k: 'struct';
    name: string;
    members: Record<string, Type>;
} | {
    k: 'void';
};
export declare function parseType(t: string): Type;
export declare function typeName(t: Type): string;
export declare function glslType(t: Type): string;
export declare class ShaderError extends AslnutError {
    readonly line: number;
    readonly col: number;
    constructor(msg: string, line: number, col: number, fix: string, where?: string);
}
interface FnSig {
    name: string;
    params: Type[];
    ret: Type;
}
export interface ScopeSpec {
    values?: Record<string, string | Type | {
        type: string | Type;
        mutable?: boolean;
        glsl?: string;
    }>;
    structs?: Record<string, Record<string, string | Type>>;
    functions?: FnSig[];
}
export interface Translated {
    glsl: string;
    functions: FnSig[];
}
export declare function translateStatements(src: string, scope: ScopeSpec, o?: {
    where?: string;
    line?: number;
    indent?: number;
}): Translated;
export declare function translateModule(src: string, scope?: ScopeSpec, o?: {
    where?: string;
    line?: number;
}): Translated;
export declare function uniformLayout(members: [string, Type][]): {
    offsets: Record<string, number>;
    size: number;
};
export {};

import type { Element } from '../../world/element.ts';

    tintAmount?: number;
    brightness?: number;
    contrast?: number;
    ground?: number;

        amount?: number;
        color?: Color;

    when?: When;
}

        map?: Record<string, string>;
        other?: string;
    }) => Scale<D, string>;
};

    readonly ramp: RampFn;
    readonly scale: ThemeScales;

}

    dark: (o?: ThemeSpec) => Theme;
    grey: (o?: ThemeSpec) => Theme;
    light: (o?: ThemeSpec) => Theme;

};

        amount: number;
        color: [number, number, number];
    } | null;
}

    key(): string;
}

## core/runtime/workers/compile/patch-policy.d.ts
import type { P2 } from '../../../geometry/networks/graph.ts';
export interface StageGate {
    stage: number;
    id: string;
    name: string;
    ok: boolean;
    counts: Record<string, number>;
    worst: Record<string, number>;
    flagged: (string | number)[];
    errors: string[];
    patches?: Patch[];
    checks?: {
        check: string;
        policy: 'repair' | 'omit' | 'stop';
        algorithm: string;
        ok: boolean;
    }[];
    locations?: {
        at: P2;
        ids: (string | number)[];
        value: number;
        metric: string;
    }[];
    surfaceLevels?: Record<string, number>;
    corrections?: {
        road: string;
        at: P2;
        s?: number;
        minimum: number;
    }[];
}
export type PatchPolicy = 'repair' | 'omit' | 'stop';
export interface Patch {
    check: string;
    policy: PatchPolicy;
    algorithm: string;
    ids: (string | number)[];
    counts: Record<string, number>;
    worst: Record<string, number>;
    moved: Record<string, unknown>;
}
export type PatchRule = PatchPolicy | ((piece: {
    check: string;
    ids: readonly (string | number)[];
}) => PatchPolicy);
export interface PatchOptions {
    patch?: Record<string, PatchRule>;
    patches?: Record<string, PatchPolicy>;
}

import { type Kernel, type Rule } from '../rules.ts';
import { Epochs, type GridSource } from '../../data/epochs.ts';
import type { Legend } from '../../encoding/scales.ts';
import { SurfaceField, type SurfaceFieldProps } from './surface.ts';
type LonLat = [number, number];
export type FieldInput = RasterField | number | ((lon: number, lat: number) => number) | Float32Array | 'terrain';
export interface FieldDraw {
    ramp?: Ramp;
    domain?: [number, number];
    opacity?: number;
    channel?: number;
    min?: number;
    on?: 'ground' | 'surface';
    base?: string;
    flat?: boolean;
    lift?: number;
}
export interface FieldProps extends ElementProps {
    kind?: 'raster';
    data?: Epochs<GridSource>;
    res: number;
    area: BBox | {
        centre: LonLat;
        size: number;
    };
    init?: number | ((lon: number, lat: number) => number) | Float32Array;
    rule?: Rule | Kernel | ((cur: number, n: {
        laplacian: number;
        neighbours: [number, number, number, number];
        inputs: Record<string, number>;
    }, dt: number, p: Record<string, number>) => number);
    inputs?: Record<string, FieldInput>;
    params?: Record<string, number>;
    rate?: number;
    dt?: number;
    draw?: FieldDraw | false;
    gpu?: 'auto' | boolean;
}
export interface Probe {
    readonly value: number;
    readonly series: readonly [number, number][];
    stop(): void;
}
export declare class RasterField extends Element<FieldProps> {
    readonly kind: 'raster';
    readonly w: number;
    readonly h: number;
    readonly cell: number;
    readonly box: BBox;
    rule: Rule;
    time: number;
    version: number;
    state: Float32Array;
    constructor(p: FieldProps);
    setProps(p: Partial<Leveled<FieldProps>>): this;
    private get drawOf();
    legend(): Legend;
    lonLatOf(i: number, j: number): LonLat;
    cellOf(lon: number, lat: number): [number, number] | null;
    get runsOn(): 'cpu' | 'gpu';
    setRule(r: FieldProps['rule'], params?: Record<string, number>): Promise<this>;
    setParams(v: Record<string, number>): this;
    reset(init?: number | Float32Array<ArrayBufferLike> | ((lon: number, lat: number) => number) | undefined): this;
    play(rate?: number): this;
    pause(): this;
    get rate(): number;
    get dt(): number;
    step(seconds?: number): this;
    sync(): Promise<void>;
    at(p: LonLat, channel?: number): number;
    values(channel?: number): Float32Array;
    probe(o: {
        at?: LonLat;
        along?: LonLat[];
        over?: LonLat[];
        every?: 'step' | number;
    }): Probe;
    attach(ctx: AttachContext): Promise<void>;
    get order(): number | undefined;
    private get surfaceDraw();
    detach(): void;
    animating(_s: State): boolean;
    frame(ctx: FrameContext): DrawCall[];
    painted(): RasterSource | null;
    status(): import("../../index.ts").ElementStatus;
    readouts(): Record<string, () => unknown>;
    epochStats(): {
        id: string;
        epochs: number;
        held: number;
        bytes: number;
        loading: number;
        loads: number;
        dropped: number;
        shown: [number, number, number];
        of: string;
    }[];
}
export declare function fieldLegend(d: {
    ramp?: Ramp;
    domain?: [number, number];
}): Legend;
export declare function field(p: Leveled<SurfaceFieldProps>): SurfaceField;
export declare function field(p: Leveled<FieldProps>): RasterField;
export {};

## core/simulation/fields/surface.d.ts
import { Element, type AttachContext, type ElementProps, type FrameContext } from '../../world/element.ts';
import type { State } from '../../world/state.ts';
import type { DrawCall } from '../../render/graphics/interface.ts';
import { Solids, type SolidInfo, type SurfaceChannel, type SurfaceChunk, type SurfaceLook, type SurfaceValues } from '../../geometry/shapes/solids.ts';
import { Epochs } from '../../data/epochs.ts';
import type { Legend } from '../../encoding/scales.ts';
import { type FieldDraw, type Probe } from './raster.ts';
export type SurfaceFacet = 'roof' | 'wall' | number;
export type SurfaceFn = (thing: SolidInfo, facet: SurfaceFacet, normal: [number, number, number], at: [number, number, number]) => number;
export type SurfaceInput = Float32Array | Record<string, Float32Array> | SurfaceFn;
export interface SurfaceDraw extends Pick<FieldDraw, 'ramp' | 'domain' | 'min'> {
    opacity?: number;
}
export interface SurfaceFieldProps extends ElementProps {
    kind: 'surface';
    on: string | Solids;
    values?: SurfaceInput;
    data?: Epochs<SurfaceInput>;
    draw?: SurfaceDraw;
    walls?: 'numbered';
}
export declare class SurfaceField extends Element<SurfaceFieldProps> implements SurfaceChannel {
    readonly kind: 'custom';
    version: number;
    time: number;
    constructor(p: SurfaceFieldProps);
    get on(): Solids | null;
    set(values: SurfaceInput): this;
    vertices(): Record<string, number | null>;
    at(p: [number, number] | [number, number, number]): number;
    probe(o: {
        at?: [number, number] | [number, number, number];
        over?: [number, number][];
        of?: string | number;
        every?: 'step' | number;
    }): Probe;
    legend(): Legend;
    attach(ctx: AttachContext): Promise<void>;
    detach(): void;
    frame(ctx: FrameContext): DrawCall[];
    readouts(): Record<string, () => unknown>;
    epochStats(): {
        id: string;
        epochs: number;
        held: number;
        bytes: number;
        loading: number;
        loads: number;
        dropped: number;
        shown: [number, number, number];
        of: string;
    }[];
    status(): import("../../index.ts").ElementStatus;
    look(s: State): SurfaceLook;
    values(c: SurfaceChunk, which: 'near' | 'far' | 'clones'): SurfaceValues | null;
}

## core/simulation/phenomena/heat/index.d.ts
import { RasterField, type FieldProps } from '../../fields/raster.ts';
import { type HeatField, type HeatInput, type HeatOptions, type HeatPoint, type HeatRasters } from './model.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../../../observation/presence.ts';
type LonLat = [number, number];
export interface PlacedHeatRasters extends HeatRasters {
    lonLat(x: number, z: number): LonLat;
    xzOf(lon: number, lat: number): [number, number];
}
export type HeatMetric = 'utci' | 'feels' | 'tmrt' | 'air' | 'surface';
export declare const HEAT_METRICS: Record<HeatMetric, {
    label: string;
    unit: string;
    range: [number, number];
}>;
export declare const HEAT_RAMP: string[];
export declare const HEAT_DIFF_RAMP: string[];
export interface HeatSummary {
    utci: number;
    feels: number;
    tmrt: number;
    air: number;
    shaded: number;
    road: number;
    points: number;
}
export interface HeatProps extends Omit<FieldProps, 'rule' | 'res' | 'area' | 'init' | 'inputs' | 'params' | 'rate' | 'dt' | 'gpu'> {
    rasters: PlacedHeatRasters;
    input: HeatInput;
    options?: Partial<HeatOptions>;
    metric?: HeatMetric;
    height?: number;
}
export declare class Heat extends RasterField {
    readonly rasters: PlacedHeatRasters;
    metric: HeatMetric;
    input: HeatInput;
    options: HeatOptions;
    summary: HeatSummary | null;
    changed: HeatSummary | null;
    busy: string;
    constructor(p: HeatProps);
    run(o?: {
        input?: HeatInput;
        options?: Partial<HeatOptions>;
        metric?: HeatMetric;
    }): Promise<HeatSummary | null>;
    compare(variant: PlacedHeatRasters, o?: {
        domain?: [number, number];
    }): Promise<HeatSummary | null>;
    point(at: LonLat): {
        p: HeatPoint;
        shade: HeatPoint | null;
    } | null;
    get modelField(): HeatField | null;
    readouts(): Record<string, () => unknown>;
}
export declare const heat: (p: Leveled<HeatProps>) => Heat;
export {};

import { MATERIALS, WALL, type Material } from './physics.ts';

export interface HeatRasters {
    w: number;
    h: number;
    cell: number;
    mat: Uint8Array;
    materials: Material[];
    bH: Float32Array;
    cTop: Float32Array;
    cBase: Float32Array;
    aw?: Float32Array;
    cTrans?: Float32Array;
    region: Uint8Array;
    walk: Uint8Array;
    regions: {
        id: string;
        traffic?: boolean;
        tallBuildings?: boolean;
        width?: number;
    }[];
}
export interface HourWeather {
    hour: number;
    ta: number;
    rh: number;
    u10: number;
    cloud: number;
    dirH: number;
    difH: number;
    precip: number;
}

    date: string;
    source: string;
    hours: HourWeather[];
}
export interface Sun {
    elevation: number;
    azimuth: number;
}
export interface HeatInput {
    hour: number;
    weather: HourWeather;
    day: DayWeather | null;
    sunAt: (hour: number) => Sun;
    key: string;
}
export interface HeatOptions {
     memory: boolean;
     surroundings: boolean;
     walls: boolean;
     rain: boolean;
     coupling: boolean;
     canyon: boolean;
     canopyTransmission: number;
}
export declare const DEFAULT_HEAT_OPTIONS: HeatOptions;

    key: keyof HeatOptions;
    label: string;
    help: string;
}[];
export { utciStress };

    nz: number;

}

    ws10: number;

## core/simulation/rules.d.ts
export interface CpuGrid {
    w: number;
    h: number;
    cell: number;
    dt: number;
    cur: Float32Array;
    inputs: Float32Array[];
    p: Float32Array;
}
export interface Rule {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly wgsl?: string;
    readonly glsl?: string;
    cpu(g: CpuGrid, i: number, j: number, out: Float32Array, o: number): void;
    readonly inputs: readonly string[];
    readonly params: Readonly<Record<string, {
        slot: number;
        value: number;
    }>>;
    maxDt(cell: number, p: Float32Array): number;
    readonly channels: readonly string[];
}
export declare const rules: {
    diffusion(o?: {
        k?: number;
    }): Rule;
    advection(o?: {
        wind?: [number, number] | 'input';
    }): Rule;
    heat(o?: {
        k?: number;
        relax?: number;
        to?: number | 'input';
        sourceScale?: number;
    }): Rule;
    shallowWater(o?: {
        friction?: number;
        gravity?: number;
        rain?: number;
        rainScale?: number;
        openEdges?: boolean;
    }): Rule;
    flow(o?: {
        friction?: number;
        gravity?: number;
        rain?: number;
        rainScale?: number;
        openEdges?: boolean;
    }): Rule;
    custom(fn: (cur: number, n: {
        laplacian: number;
        neighbours: [number, number, number, number];
        inputs: Record<string, number>;
    }, dt: number, p: Record<string, number>) => number, o?: {
        params?: Record<string, number>;
        inputs?: string[];
        maxDt?: number;
    }): Rule;
};
export interface Kernel {
    wgsl: string;
    glsl?: string;
    cpu?: (cur: number, n: {
        laplacian: number;
        neighbours: [number, number, number, number];
        inputs: Record<string, number>;
    }, dt: number, p: Record<string, number>) => number;
    params?: Record<string, number>;
    inputs?: string[];
    maxDt?: number;
}
export declare function kernel(k: Kernel): Rule;
export declare function ruleOf(r: Rule | Kernel | ((cur: number, n: Parameters<NonNullable<Kernel['cpu']>>[1], dt: number, p: Record<string, number>) => number)): Rule;

## core/space/groups.d.ts
import { Element, type AttachContext, type ElementProps, type ElementStatus, type FrameContext } from '../world/element.ts';
import type { DrawCall } from '../render/graphics/interface.ts';
import { Source, type SourceContext } from '../data/sources/source.ts';
import { type Feature, type Props } from '../data/features.ts';
import { Log } from '../data/history.ts';
import { type Channel } from '../data/channels.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../observation/presence.ts';
export type GroupBy<D> = {
    regions: Source<any> | readonly Feature[];
    name?: (f: Feature) => string;
} | {
    cells: number;
} | ((d: D) => string | number | null | undefined);
export type GroupValue<D> = {
    count: true;
} | {
    sum: Channel<D>;
} | {
    mean: Channel<D>;
} | {
    min: Channel<D>;
} | {
    max: Channel<D>;
} | {
    of: Channel<D>;
    fn: (values: number[]) => number;
};
export interface GroupsProps<D> extends ElementProps {
    of: string | Element | Source<any> | readonly D[];
    by: GroupBy<D> | Record<string, GroupBy<D>>;
    values?: Record<string, GroupValue<D>>;
    log?: Log;
    show?: {
        levels: string[];
    };
    mark?: (g: GroupsSource, level: string | null) => Element;
}
export interface GroupsJobIn {
    n: number;
    off: Int32Array;
    t: Float64Array;
    x: Float64Array;
    y: Float64Array;
    by: {
        kind: 'regions';
        groups: number;
        rings: Float64Array;
        ringOff: Int32Array;
        groupRings: Int32Array;
        hole: Uint8Array;
        box: Float64Array;
    } | {
        kind: 'cells';
        size: number;
        lat0: number;
    } | {
        kind: 'keys';
        key: Int32Array;
        groups: number;
    };
    ops: number[];
    chan: number[];
    vOff: Int32Array;
    vT: Float64Array;
    v: Float32Array[];
}
export interface GroupsJobOut {
    groups: number;
    cells: Int32Array;
    members: Int32Array;
    off: Int32Array;
    t: Float64Array;
    v: Float32Array[];
}
export declare function groupsJob(I: GroupsJobIn, fns?: (((xs: number[]) => number) | null)[]): GroupsJobOut;
export declare class GroupsSource extends Source<Props> {
    readonly level: string | null;
    log: Log;
    constructor(id: string, level: string | null, make: (ctx: SourceContext) => Promise<{
        features: Feature[];
        log: Log;
    }>);
    protected load(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
    again(ctx: SourceContext): Promise<void>;
}
export declare class Groups<D = unknown> extends Element<GroupsProps<D>> {
    readonly kind: 'custom';
    readonly sources: GroupsSource[];
    constructor(p: GroupsProps<D>);
    companions(): Element[];
    attach(ctx: AttachContext): Promise<void>;
    detach(): void;
    frame(_ctx: FrameContext): DrawCall[];
    status(): ElementStatus;
    readouts(): {
        groups: () => string;
    };
}
export declare function groups<D = unknown>(p: Leveled<GroupsProps<D>>): Groups<D>;

## core/space/regions.d.ts
import type { Loader } from '../data/loading/loader.ts';
import { type Feature, type Props } from '../data/features.ts';
import { Rows, type Source } from '../data/sources/index.ts';
export interface Region {
    id: string | number;
    level: string;
    name: string;
    props: Props;
    parent: string | number | null;
    feature: Feature;
}
export interface TreeOptions {
    levels: string[];
    sources: Record<string, Source>;
    parentOf?: Record<string, (f: Feature) => string | number | null | undefined>;
    nameOf?: (f: Feature, level: string) => string;
}
export interface RollupOptions {
    on: string;
    to: string;
    from?: string;
    sum?: string[];
    mean?: string[];
    count?: boolean;
    fn?: (rows: Props[], region: Region) => Props;
}
export declare class RegionTree {
    readonly levels: string[];
    constructor(o: TreeOptions);
    load(loader: Loader): Promise<this>;
    build(): void;
    at(lon: number, lat: number, level?: string): Region | null;
    get(level: string, id: string | number): Region | null;
    parent(r: Region): Region | null;
    children(r: Region): Region[];
    path(r: Region): Region[];
    regionsOf(level: string): Region[];
    level(level: string): Source;
    rollup(rows: Source<any>, o: RollupOptions): Rows;
}
export declare const regions: {
    tree: (o: TreeOptions) => RegionTree;
};

## core/world/element.d.ts
import type { Loader } from '../data/loading/loader.ts';
import type { Camera } from '../observation/observers/orbit/camera.ts';
import type { DrawCall, GBuffer, GTexture, Graphics, GroundDraw } from '../render/graphics/interface.ts';
import type { Light } from '../render/look/light.ts';
import type { Profile } from '../render/graphics/caps.ts';
import { type ParamBlock, type ParamValue, type ShaderCode } from '../render/graphics/shaders/hooks.ts';
import type { LonLatH } from '../space/geo.ts';
import type { Kind } from './order.ts';
import type { State } from './state.ts';
import { type Leveled, type LevelValues, type When } from '../observation/presence.ts';
export interface ParamSpec {
    type: 'number' | 'enum' | 'bool' | 'color' | 'vector' | 'text';
    default: unknown;
    range?: [number, number];
    options?: string[];
    unit?: string;
    describe?: string;
}
export interface ParamDef extends ParamSpec {
    get(): unknown;
    set(v: unknown): void;
}
export interface ElementProps {
    id?: string;
    when?: When;
    order?: number;
    above?: string;
    below?: string;
    transition?: number;
    opacity?: number;
    label?: string;
    params?: Record<string, ParamValue>;
    shader?: ShaderCode;
    selected?: FormVisual | false;
    subject?: boolean;
}
export interface FormVisual {
    shape?: (d: any, place: ThingPlace) => {
        levels: readonly unknown[];
    } | null;
    material?: import('../render/look/materials.ts').MaterialName | import('../render/look/materials.ts').Material;
    follow?: boolean;
}
export interface ThingPlace {
    at: [number, number, number];
    turn: number;
    scale: [number, number, number];
    radius?: number;
}
export interface AttachContext {
    graphics: Graphics | null;
    loader: Loader;
    profile: Profile | null;
    camera: Camera;
    world: WorldLike;
}
export interface Picked<D = unknown> {
    element: string;
    id: string | number;
    props: Record<string, unknown>;
    d: D;
    index: number;
    at: LonLatH | null;
    x: number;
    y: number;
}
export interface Sea {
    level: number;
    color: readonly [number, number, number];
    look?: {
        block: import('../render/graphics/interface.ts').GBuffer;
        field: import('../render/graphics/interface.ts').GTexture;
    } | null;
}
export interface FrameContext {
    labelRoom?: [number, number, number, number][];
    sea?: Sea | null;
    graphics: Graphics;
    camera: Camera;
    s: State;
    frame: GBuffer;
    now: number;
    dt: number;
    world: WorldLike;
    n?: number;
    loadNow?: number;
    shadowMap?: GTexture;
    shadowView?: Float32Array | null;
}
export interface WorldLike {
    readonly id: string;
    heightAt(lon: number, lat: number): number;
    readonly exaggeration: number;
    error(e: unknown): void;
    readonly graphicsApi: string;
    readonly groundMarks?: 'prisms' | 'tiles';
    readonly groundMarksChosen?: boolean;
    rangeIn?(w: number, s: number, e: number, n: number): [number, number] | null;
    readonly groundVersion: number;
    readonly context: 'app' | 'video';
    readonly now: number;
    elementsOfKind(kind: Kind): readonly Element[];
    prepareGround?(ground: GroundDraw[], ctx: FrameContext): boolean;
    readonly light?: Light | null;
    readonly standing?: boolean;
    topIn?(w: number, s: number, e: number, n: number): number | null;
    elementChanged?(l: Element, keys: string[]): void;
    selectedBy?(id: string): string | undefined;
    readonly themeNow?: import('../render/style/theme.ts').ThemeNow | null;
}
export interface ElementStatus {
    ready: boolean;
    pending: string[];
    failed: {
        what: string;
        error: string;
    }[];
    missing: string[];
}
export declare abstract class Element<P extends ElementProps = ElementProps> {
    abstract readonly kind: Kind;
    readonly id: string;
    props: P;
    added: number;
    shown: number;
    target: number;
    protected paramBlock: ParamBlock | null;
    protected paramValues: Record<string, ParamValue>;
    protected paramsDirty: boolean;
    failure: Error | null;
    forced: boolean | null;
    pickNo: number;
    once: number | null;
    detailPinned: boolean;
    detailOwn: number;
    detailOf(s: State): number;
    protected attached: AttachContext | null;
    readonly byLevel: Readonly<Record<string, LevelValues<unknown>>>;
    constructor(props: Leveled<P>);
    atLevel(names: readonly string[], index: number): string[];
    protected get levelNow(): string | null;
    get order(): number | undefined;
    get fades(): boolean;
    get sits(): 'ground' | 'over' | 'above' | 'free' | null;
    get above(): string | undefined;
    get below(): string | undefined;
    params(): Record<string, ParamDef>;
    setParam(name: string, v: ParamValue): void;
    revealSeconds(): number;
    pickInfo(_index: number): Omit<Picked, 'at' | 'x' | 'y'> | null;
    groundPick?(lon: number, lat: number): number | null;
    hovered(_ids: Set<string> | null): void;
    placeOf(_picked: Picked): ThingPlace | null;
    companions?(): Element[];
    restacked?(k: number): void;
    owner?: Element | null;
    sea?(s: State): Sea | null;
    light?(s: State): Light | null;
    animating(_s: State): boolean;
    wantsFrames(s: State): number;
    readouts(): Record<string, () => unknown>;
    points(): Record<string, () => LonLatH>;
    attach(ctx: AttachContext): Promise<void>;
    prepare(_ctx: FrameContext): boolean;
    letGo?(): void;
    abstract frame(ctx: FrameContext): DrawCall[];
    status(): ElementStatus;
    detach(): void;
    setProps(p: Partial<Leveled<P>>): this;
    attribution(): string[];
}

export type ErrorCode = 'E-GPU-001' | 'E-GPU-002' | 'E-GPU-003' | 'E-GPU-004' | 'E-GPU-005' | 'E-SHADER-001' | 'E-WORLD-001' | 'E-WORLD-002' | 'E-WORLD-003' | 'E-WORLD-004' | 'E-WORLD-005' | 'E-WORLD-006' | 'E-LEVEL-001' | 'E-LEVEL-002' | 'E-WHEN-001' | 'E-VIEW-001' | 'E-SETTLE-001' | 'E-SETTLE-002' | 'E-LOAD-001' | 'E-LOAD-002' | 'E-LOAD-003' | 'E-LOAD-004' | 'E-LOAD-005' | 'E-DATA-001' | 'E-DATA-002' | 'E-DATA-003' | 'E-DATA-004' | 'E-LOAD-006' | 'E-STATE-001' | 'E-LANE-001' | 'E-LANE-002' | 'E-COMPAT-001' | 'E-COMPAT-002' | 'E-COMPAT-003' | 'E-COMPAT-004' | 'E-COMPAT-005' | 'E-COMPAT-006';

    readonly fix: string;
    readonly where: string;

        fix?: string;
        where?: string;
        details?: unknown;
        cause?: unknown;
    });
}

        webgl2: boolean;
        reason: string;
    };

        webgpu: boolean;
        webgl2: boolean;
        reason: string;
    });
}

## kits/dashboard/cards.d.ts
import { Element, type AttachContext, type ElementProps, type ElementStatus, type FrameContext, type Picked } from '../../core/world/element.ts';
import type { State } from '../../core/world/state.ts';
import type { DrawCall } from '../../core/render/graphics/interface.ts';
import { Log } from '../../core/data/history.ts';
import { type Channel } from '../../core/data/channels.ts';
import { type PlacedOn, type PlacementOptions } from '../../core/entities/placement.ts';
import { IconAtlas } from '../../core/encoding/marks/icon.ts';
import { type Color } from '../../core/render/style/color.ts';
import { type SizeOf } from '../../core/render/drawing/size.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../../core/observation/presence.ts';
export interface CardRow<D> {
    label: string;
    value?: Channel<D>;
    text?: (d: D) => string | number | null | undefined;
    of?: Channel<D>;
    decimals?: 0 | 1 | 2;
    bar?: boolean | {
        domain: [number, number];
    };
}
export interface CardsCollide {
    cover?: number;
    hold?: number;
    away?: number;
    fade?: number;
}
export interface CardLook {
    background?: Color;
    ink?: Color;
    inkOnDark?: Color;
    bar?: Color;
    badge?: Color;
    radius?: number;
    dots?: boolean;
}
export interface CardsProps<D> extends ElementProps, Omit<PlacementOptions<D>, 'heading'> {
    of: PlacedOn<D>;
    log?: Log;
    icons?: IconAtlas;
    header?: {
        icon?: string | ((d: D) => string | null | undefined);
        text?: string | ((d: D) => string | number | null | undefined);
    };
    rows?: CardRow<D>[];
    fill?: {
        value: Channel<D>;
        color: (v: number) => Color;
        domain?: [number, number];
    };
    badge?: {
        value: Channel<D>;
        when?: (v: number, d: D) => boolean;
    };
    size?: SizeOf<number>;
    fit?: boolean | {
        min?: number;
        max?: number;
    };
    collide?: boolean | CardsCollide;
    priority?: (d: D) => number;
    look?: CardLook;
    tooltip?: (d: D, s: State) => string | null | undefined;
    onTap?: (f: Picked<D>, s: State) => void;
    onHover?: (f: Picked<D> | null, s: State) => void;
    select?: 'tap' | 'toggle' | false;
    pickable?: boolean;
}
export declare class Cards<D = unknown> extends Element<CardsProps<D>> {
    readonly kind: 'point';
    cols: Log | null;
    constructor(p: CardsProps<D>);
    attach(ctx: AttachContext): Promise<void>;
    setProps(given: Partial<Leveled<CardsProps<D>>>): this;
    private get slots();
    frame(ctx: FrameContext): DrawCall[];
    decisions(): Promise<Float32Array | null>;
    cardPlace(i: number): [number, number] | null;
    get ids(): readonly string[];
    valuesOf(i: number, t?: number): Record<string, number>;
    pickInfo(index: number): Omit<Picked, 'at' | 'x' | 'y'> | null;
    status(): ElementStatus;
    wantsFrames(s: State): number;
    readouts(): {
        cards: () => number;
        samples: () => number;
        channels: () => number;
    };
    detach(): void;
}
export declare function cards<D = unknown>(p: Leveled<CardsProps<D>>): Cards<D>;

import type { Made } from '../../core/geometry/shapes/shape.ts';

export interface AvatarOptions {
    start?: (at: [number, number]) => {
        at: [number, number];
        heading: number;
    } | null;
    onMode?: (m: AvatarMode) => void;
    touch?: boolean;
    obstacle?: (lon: number, lat: number) => number;
    colors?: {
        shirt?: string;
        pants?: string;
        skin?: string;
        car?: string;
        cabin?: string;
        drone?: string;
    };
    shapes?: {
        walker?: (stride: number) => Made;
        car?: () => Made;
        drone?: () => Made;
    };
    body?: AvatarBody | null;
    camera?: 'follow' | 'first-person';
    vehicles?: {
        near: () => {
            id: string;
            at: [number, number];
            heading: number;
            made: Made;
        }[];
        onEnter?: (id: string) => void;
        onExit?: (v: {
            id: string;
            at: [number, number];
            heading: number;
            made: Made;
        }) => void;
    };
}
export interface AvatarBody {
    figure: () => Figure | null;
    palette?: readonly (string | number)[];
    decal?: readonly [number, number, number];
    scale?: number;
}
export declare class Avatars {
    readonly element: EntityElement;
    mode: AvatarMode;
    readonly me: Entity;

    setBody(b: AvatarBody | null): this;
    setCamera(v: 'follow' | 'first-person'): this;
    riding: {
        id: string;
        made: Made;
    } | null;
    enterOrExit(): boolean;
    get kmh(): number;
    setMode(next: AvatarMode): this;
    dispose(): void;
}

import { MeshBuilder, type Colorish } from '../../../../../../core/geometry/shapes/mesh.ts';
import { Solids, type SolidsProps } from '../../../../../../core/geometry/shapes/solids.ts';
import { Source } from '../../../../../../core/data/sources/source.ts';

import { type BlockLook, type HouseLook, type RoofShape } from './index.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../../../../../../core/observation/presence.ts';
export type RoofKind = 'flat' | 'gable' | 'hip' | 'pyramidal' | 'skillion' | 'dome';
export type { RoofShape };
export type Roof = RoofKind | RoofShape;
export interface BuildingLook {
    wall: string;
    roof: string;
    window: string;
}
export interface BuildingsProps extends Omit<SolidsProps, 'windows'> {
    data: Source | Feature[];
    height?: (d: Feature) => number;
    floor?: number;
    fallback?: number;
    look?: ((d: Feature) => Partial<BuildingLook>) | {
        block?: BlockLook;
        house?: HouseLook;
    };
    house?: (d: Feature, area: number, height: number) => boolean;
    front?: (d: Feature) => number | null | undefined;
    roofs?: boolean | ((d: Feature) => Roof | null | undefined);
    pitch?: number;
    windows?: false | {
        lit?: number | ((s: import('../../../../../../core/world/state.ts').State) => number);
        interior?: boolean;
    };
    sink?: number;
}
export declare function roofOf(props: Record<string, unknown>): RoofKind | null;

import { type ClampJson, type UnderOptions } from '../../../../../../core/geometry/networks/clamp-index.ts';
import { StreetDetail, type StreetDetailProps } from '../../../../surface/street-detail.ts';
import type { Terrain } from '../../../../surface/sources.ts';
import type { Piece } from '../../../../../../core/geometry/networks/bake-types.ts';
import type { WorkerLane } from '../../../../../../core/runtime/workers.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../../../../../../core/observation/presence.ts';
export interface BakedTile {
    v: number;
    ix: number;
    iz: number;
    size: number;
    infos: {
        kind: string;
        props: Record<string, unknown>;
        patchRefs?: number[];
    }[];
    pieces: number[][];
    numeric?: string[];
    numericScale?: number;
    turnUnits?: number;
    overhead?: number[];
    levels?: [number, number][];
}
export type RoadsLook = Record<string, string>;
export declare const BAKED_LOOK: RoadsLook;
export interface BakedRoadsProps extends Omit<StreetDetailProps<BakedTile, null>, 'build'> {
    look?: Partial<RoadsLook>;
    hide?: string[];
    worker?: WorkerLane;
}
export type BakedNumbers = Pick<BakedTile, 'v' | 'ix' | 'iz' | 'size' | 'numeric' | 'numericScale' | 'turnUnits'> & {
    pieces?: number[][];
};
export declare function bakedRoadsWorker(): {
    roads: (t: BakedNumbers, kinds: string[], look: RoadsLook, hide: string[]) => Float32Array;
    clamp: (job: ClampJob) => Float32Array;
};
export declare function bakedPieces(t: BakedTile, kinds: readonly string[]): Piece[];
export declare function bakedMesh(t: BakedTile, kinds: readonly string[], lookIn: RoadsLook, hide?: ReadonlySet<string>): {
    mesh: Float32Array;
    ids: number;
};
export declare function bakedStands(t: BakedTile, kinds: readonly string[], hide?: ReadonlySet<string>): Float32Array;
export declare class BakedRoads extends StreetDetail<BakedTile, null> {
    constructor(p: BakedRoadsProps);
    standVersion: number;
    put(key: string, spec: Parameters<StreetDetail['put']>[1]): this;
    drop(key: string): this;
    levelsAt(lon: number, lat: number, out: number[]): void;
}
export declare const bakedRoads: (p: Leveled<BakedRoadsProps>) => BakedRoads;
export interface ClampJob {
    out: Float32Array;
    t: {
        z: number;
        x: number;
        y: number;
    };
    n: number;
    at: ClampAt;
    keys: string[];
    tiles: ({
        url: string;
        j: ClampJson;
    } | null)[];
    o: ClampOptions;
}
interface ClampAt {
    origin: [number, number];
    size: number;
    kx: number;
}
interface ClampOptions {
    batter: number;
    reach: number;
    legacy: boolean;
    under: {
        margin?: number;
        steep?: number;
        coarse?: number;
    } | null;
}
export declare function clampTerrain(base: Terrain, url: string, o?: {
    batter?: number;
    reach?: number;
    legacy?: boolean;
    under?: UnderOptions | false;

}): Terrain;

        type: string;
        coordinates: unknown;
    };
}
export declare function roadFeatures(features: RoadFeature[]): {
    ways: RoadWay[];
    crossings: {
        at: P2;
        tags: Record<string, string>;
    }[];
};
export declare const pathFeatures: (features: RoadFeature[]) => {
    pts: P2[];
    tags: Record<string, string>;
}[];
export declare const reserveFeatures: (features: RoadFeature[]) => P2[][];
export declare const waterFeatures: (features: RoadFeature[]) => {
    id: string | number;
    pts: P2[];
    tags: Record<string, string>;
}[];

    };
}

    crownRadius: number;
    crownBase: number;
    transmissivity: number;
}

    z: number;
    height: number;
    radius: number;
    id: number;
    seed: number;
    species?: string | BranchSpec;
}

    shape: string;

}[];

    base: number;
    radius: number;

## modules/world/earth/shared/plan.d.ts
export type P2 = [number, number];
export declare function signedArea(r: readonly P2[]): number;
export declare function densify(pts: readonly P2[], step: number): P2[];
export declare function orientedBox(ring: readonly P2[]): {
    cx: number;
    cz: number;
    a: number;
    b: number;
    ux: number;
    uz: number;
    vx: number;
    vz: number;
};
export declare function nearestOn<S extends {
    ax: number;
    az: number;
    bx: number;
    bz: number;
}>(x: number, z: number, segs: readonly S[]): {
    px: number;
    pz: number;
    d: number;
    tx: number;
    tz: number;
    seg: S;
} | null;
export declare function inside(x: number, z: number, ring: readonly P2[]): boolean;
export declare class Rng {
    constructor(seed: number);
    next(): number;
    range(a: number, b: number): number;
    chance(p: number): boolean;
    pick<T>(xs: readonly T[]): T;
    weighted<T extends string>(w: Readonly<Record<T, number>>): T;
}
export declare const seedOf: (...xs: number[]) => number;
export declare class Footprints {
    addRing(ring: readonly P2[]): this;
    addBand(line: readonly P2[], d0: number, d1: number): this;
    blocked(x: number, z: number, r?: number): boolean;
    place(x: number, z: number, o?: {
        r?: number;
        nudge?: number;
    }): P2 | null;
}

export interface CityTile {
    v: number;
    ix: number;
    iz: number;
    size: number;
    s: string[];
    roads: number[][];
    buildings: number[][];
    trees: number[];
    parks: number[][];
    water: number[][];
    streams: number[][];
    reserves?: number[][];
    junctions?: number[][];
    baked?: boolean;
    fronts?: number[][];
    anchors?: number[];
    pads?: number[];
    seeds?: number[];
    tseeds?: number[];
    homes?: number[];
    lots?: number[][];
    furniture?: number[];
    patches?: number[][];
    omitted?: number[][];
}
export interface CityLook {
    street: StreetLook;
    house: HouseLook;
    block: BlockLook;
    lot: LotLook;
    trees: TreeLook;
    poles: PoleLook;

    sports: string;

    pad?: string;

        into(mb: MeshBuilder, site: HouseSite, o: {
            at?: [number, number, number];

        }): unknown;
    };

}

}

    }[];

        z: number;

    }[];

        building: number;
        ring: P2[];
    }[];

        what: 'building' | 'plot' | 'tree' | 'furniture';
        x: number;
        z: number;
        check: string;
        id: string;
    }[];

        x: number;
        z: number;
        id: number;
        top: number;
    }[];

        name: string;
        group: string;

        id: number;
    }[];

}

    readonly speed: 6;
    readonly from: 225;
    readonly glitter: 1;
    readonly waves: 16;
    readonly reach: 300;
};
export interface Wave {
    dir: [number, number];
    k: number;
    a: number;
    omega: number;
    q: number;
}
export declare function waveSet(o: {
    speed: number;
    from: number;
    swell?: {
        height?: number;
        period?: number;
        from?: number;
    } | false;
    count?: number;
    seed?: number;
}): Wave[];
export declare const rgbOf: (c: string) => [number, number, number];

## modules/world/surface/sources.d.ts
import type { Loader, Priority } from '../../../core/data/loading/loader.ts';
import { type Terms } from '../../../core/data/rights.ts';
import type { When } from '../../../core/observation/presence.ts';
import { type BBox } from '../../../core/space/geo.ts';
export declare const TILE: number;
export type Ctx2D = OffscreenCanvasRenderingContext2D;
export interface Tile {
    z: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
}
export interface SourceContext {
    loader: Loader;
    priority: Priority;
    prefetch: boolean;
    signal?: AbortSignal;
}
export type Blend = 'normal' | 'multiply' | 'screen' | 'add';
interface Common {
    id: string;
    attribution: string;
    terms: Terms;
    when?: When;
    where?: BBox;
}
export interface Imagery extends Common {
    readonly kind: 'imagery';
    minZoom: number;
    maxZoom: number;
    opacity: number;
    blend: Blend;
    version: number;
    draw(g: Ctx2D, tile: Tile, ctx: SourceContext): Promise<boolean>;
    bitmap?(tile: Tile, ctx: SourceContext): Promise<{
        im: ImageBitmap;
        sx: number;
        sy: number;
        size: number;
    } | null>;
    pixels?(tile: Tile, ctx: SourceContext): Promise<{
        width: number;
        height: number;
        data: Uint8ClampedArray;
    } | null>;
    opaque?: boolean;
    urlFor?(tile: Tile): string | null;
}
export interface Terrain extends Common {
    readonly kind: 'terrain';
    maxZoom: number;
    blend: number;
    version: number;
    heights(tile: Tile, n: number, ctx: SourceContext): Promise<Float32Array | null>;
    urlFor?(tile: Tile): string | null;
}
export interface CommonOptions {
    id?: string;
    attribution?: string;
    terms?: Partial<Terms>;
    when?: When;
    where?: BBox;
}
export declare function checkBBox(b: unknown, where: string): BBox | undefined;
export declare const covers: (s: {
    where?: BBox;
}, t: Tile) => boolean;
export interface XYZOptions extends CommonOptions {
    url: string;
    minZoom?: number;
    maxZoom?: number;
    opacity?: number;
    blend?: Blend;
    subdomains?: string[];
    opaque?: boolean;
}
export declare const COMPOSITE: Record<Blend, GlobalCompositeOperation>;
export declare function xyzUrl(tpl: string, t: Tile, subdomains?: string[]): string;
export declare function xyzImagery(o: XYZOptions & {
    terms?: Partial<Terms>;
}, base?: Readonly<Terms>): Imagery;
export declare function paint<T>(g: Ctx2D, s: {
    where?: BBox;
}, t: Tile, f: () => T | Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
export type Encoding = 'terrarium' | 'mapbox';
export declare const decodeHeight: (enc: Encoding, r: number, g: number, b: number) => number;
export declare function sampleRGB(px: Uint8ClampedArray | Uint8Array, w: number, enc: Encoding, k: number, ox: number, oy: number, n: number): Float32Array;
export interface RGBTerrainOptions extends CommonOptions {
    url: string;
    encoding?: Encoding;
    maxZoom?: number;
    blend?: number;
    coarser?: number;
}
export declare function rgbTerrain(o: RGBTerrainOptions, base?: Readonly<Terms>): Terrain;
export interface GridData {
    cols: number;
    rows: number;
    heights: Float32Array;
    bbox?: BBox | {
        west: number;
        south: number;
        east: number;
        north: number;
    };
    local?: {
        lon0: number;
        lat0: number;
        west: number;
        north: number;
        cell: number;
    };
    nodata?: number;
}
export declare function gridSampler(d: GridData, where: string): {
    at(lon: number, lat: number): number;
    box: BBox;
};
export declare function tileHeights(t: Tile, n: number, at: (lon: number, lat: number) => number): Float32Array;
export declare function composeHeights(list: Terrain[], t: Tile, n: number, ctx: SourceContext): Promise<Float32Array | null>;
export {};

import { Solids, type SolidsChunk, type SolidsProps } from '../../../core/geometry/shapes/solids.ts';
import type { AttachContext, FrameContext, ElementStatus } from '../../../core/world/element.ts';
import type { WorkerLane } from '../../../core/runtime/workers.ts';
import type { Leveled } from '../../../core/observation/presence.ts';
export interface DetailIndex {
    size: number;
    origin: [number, number];
    tiles: [number, number][];
    attribution?: string;
    sources?: Record<string, {
        title: string;
        licence: string;
        url: string;
    }>;
}
export interface DetailContext {
    ix: number;
    iz: number;
    size: number;
    origin: [number, number];
    at: [number, number];
    groundAt(x: number, z: number): number;
    lonLat(x: number, z: number): [number, number];
}
export interface DetailBuilt<E = unknown> {
    chunk: Omit<SolidsChunk, 'origin' | 'at'>;
    extra?: E;
}
export interface StreetDetailProps<T, E> extends SolidsProps {
    url: string | DetailIndex;
    tileUrl?: (ix: number, iz: number) => string;
    build: (tile: T, ctx: DetailContext) => DetailBuilt<E> | Promise<DetailBuilt<E>>;
    near?: number;
    fetches?: number;
    budgetMs?: number;
    worker?: WorkerLane;
    putMs?: number;
    onTile?: (key: string, extra: E | null, ctx: DetailContext | null) => void;
}
export declare class StreetDetail<T = unknown, E = unknown> extends Solids {
    readonly stands = false;
    props: StreetDetailProps<T, E>;
    constructor(p: StreetDetailProps<T, E>);
    attach(ctx: AttachContext): Promise<void>;
    built(): {
        key: string;
        extra: E | undefined;
        ctx: DetailContext;
    }[];
    tiles(): {
        key: string;
        tile: T;
        ctx: DetailContext;
    }[];
    builtTile(ix: number, iz: number): T | null;
    rebuild(): this;
    get area(): DetailIndex | null;
    frame(ctx: FrameContext): ReturnType<Solids['frame']>;
    status(): ElementStatus;
    animating(): boolean;
    attribution(): string[];
    tileCounts(): {
        loading: number;
        ready: number;
        built: number;
        available: number;
    };
}
export declare const streetDetail: <T, E>(p: Leveled<StreetDetailProps<T, E>>) => StreetDetail<T, E>;

import { Element, type AttachContext, type FrameContext, type ElementProps, type ElementStatus } from '../../../core/world/element.ts';

    };

    status(): ElementStatus;

        moving: number;
        still: number;
    };
    prepareOverlays(ground: GroundDraw[], ctx: FrameContext, pixelRatio: number): boolean;
    overlays(ground: GroundDraw[], ctx: FrameContext, pixelRatio: number, all: boolean): boolean;
    tileStats(): {
        holes: number;

        standIns: number;
        visible: number;
        loading: number;
        cached: number;
        zMin: number;

## tools/catalog.d.ts
import type { PackageInfo } from '../core/geometry/shapes/package.ts';
export { form, type FormControl, type FormSource, type KnobSpec } from '../core/geometry/shapes/shape.ts';
export type AssetKind = 'shape' | 'element';
export declare const ASSET_KINDS: readonly AssetKind[];
export interface CatalogEntry {
    id: string;
    kind: AssetKind;
    name: string;
    licence: string;
    path: string;
    tags?: string[];
    version?: string;
    author?: string;
    about?: string;
    thumbs?: Record<string, string>;
    presets?: string[];
}
export interface CatalogFile {
    version: 1;
    entries: CatalogEntry[];
}
export interface CatalogQuery {
    text?: string;
    kind?: AssetKind;
    tag?: string | string[];
    licence?: string | string[];
}
export declare function safePath(p: string): string;
export declare class Catalog {
    readonly entries: readonly CatalogEntry[];
    constructor(entries: CatalogEntry[]);
    search(q?: string | CatalogQuery): CatalogEntry[];
    get(id: string): CatalogEntry | undefined;
    tags(): string[];
    licences(): string[];
    toJSON(): CatalogFile;
}
export declare function catalog(from?: CatalogEntry[] | CatalogFile | string): Catalog;
export declare function entryOf(info: PackageInfo, o: {
    kind: AssetKind;
    path: string;
    presets?: string[];
}): CatalogEntry;
export declare const CODE_FILES: RegExp;
export declare const typeOf: (path: string) => string;
export declare function kindOfFiles(paths: Iterable<string>): AssetKind | null;
export declare function packAsset(files: Record<string, Uint8Array | ArrayBuffer | string>, o: {
    id: string;
    kind?: AssetKind;
}): Blob;
export interface UnpackedAsset {
    id: string;
    kind: AssetKind | null;
    created: number;
    files: Record<string, Uint8Array>;
    info: PackageInfo | null;
    licence: string | null;
    licenceText: string | null;
    code: string[];
    data: string[];
    trust: 'data' | 'code';
    text(path: string): string;
}
export declare function unpackAsset(file: Blob | ArrayBuffer, o?: {
    code?: RegExp;
    maxBytes?: number;
    maxFiles?: number;
}): Promise<UnpackedAsset>;
